*Takudzwa Togarepi,Gaspard Quenard,Damien Pellier,Humbert Fiorino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对HTN规划中计划去序（plan deordering）研究不足的问题，将经典规划中两种成熟的去序技术扩展到HTN设置，考虑层级分解约束，并在IPC 2023 Partial-Order HTN基准上与Optiplan对比，结果显示两种实现在序约束数量上显著减少，关键路径长度也有改善但幅度较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTN规划虽被广泛研究，但多数文献聚焦于计划生成，对计划后优化关注较少。计划去序在经典规划中已被广泛研究，但在HTN设置下仍缺乏研究。因此需要将经典规划中的去序技术适配到HTN框架中，以移除不必要的序约束，提高计划的并行性和灵活性。

Method: 作者将经典规划中两种成熟的计划去序技术进行扩展，使其能够处理HTN规划的层级分解约束，从而在保持HTN计划有效性的前提下移除动作间不必要的顺序约束。

Result: 在IPC 2023 Partial-Order HTN基准上的实验表明，两种实现均显著减少了序约束的数量；同时关键路径长度也有一定下降，但改善幅度不如序约束的减少那么显著。

Conclusion: 适配经典去序技术到HTN规划是可行且有效的，能大幅减少计划中的序约束。尽管关键路径长度的改善相对有限，但整体方法为HTN计划后优化提供了有价值的实践路径。

Abstract: Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) planning is a powerful planning formalism based on task decomposition. Although most of the literature studied plan generation, comparatively less attention has been paid to post-plan optimization. In particular, plan deordering has been extensively studied in classical planning but remains under-researched in the HTN setting. Plan deordering removes unnecessary ordering constraints between actions in a plan whilst keeping the plan valid. In this paper, we adapt two established plan deordering techniques from classical planning by extending the techniques to account for hierarchical decomposition constraints. We evaluate our proposed approaches on the IPC 2023 Partial-Order HTN benchmarks and we compare them against Optiplan, an HTN planner that generates partially ordered plans directly. Our results show a substantial reduction in number of ordering constraints in both our implementations. Although we also observe a reduction in critical path length, the improvements are less pronounced.

</details>


### [44] [Value-Preserving Architectures for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03920)
*Alessandro Pesare,Tommaso Dolci,Katja Hose,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中，架构设计如何影响以人为本的价值观（如隐私、公平、安全），提出了三类价值保持的架构模式：联邦拓扑的隐私感知架构、分布式拓扑促进多元化的架构、以及守护代理（guard-agent）架构用于检测和缓解不公平性，并通过实际用例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI和基于LLM的多智能体系统的兴起，复杂任务自动化带来机遇的同时也引发了隐私、公平、安全等基本人类价值观遭受侵蚀的担忧。传统软件工程聚焦于功能正确性，而LLM和AI智能体融入复杂社会技术系统后，迫切需要负责任的软件工程和稳健的价值对齐。尽管架构决策塑造系统行为，但现有研究较少系统性地将架构设计与人类中心价值观联系起来。

Method: 论文通过论证性分析方法，首先指出MAS中的架构设计决策（如协调机制、通信协议、系统拓扑）不仅影响功能与性能，还能促进价值导向的系统行为。随后提出三类价值保持的架构模式：(i) 联邦拓扑的隐私感知架构；(ii) 促进多元化与多样性的分布式架构；(iii) 用于检测和缓解不公平的守护代理架构。最后通过代表性用例在现实场景中说明各架构的有效性。

Result: 本文系统性地将MAS架构设计与具体人类中心价值观（隐私、多元性/多样性、公平）进行了映射，提出了一套可操作的架构模式，并通过真实世界用例验证了这些模式在不同场景下的适用性与价值对齐效果。该工作为可信赖MAS的设计提供了统一架构模式和指导方针的基础。

Conclusion: 论文主张架构选择是MAS价值对齐的关键杠杆，通过将架构设计与人类中心价值观建立显式关联，为设计可信赖的MAS奠定了统一架构模式与指南的基础，推动负责任的软件工程在智能体系统中的应用。

Abstract: The emergence of agentic AI and LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) presents unprecedented opportunities for automating complex tasks, while simultaneously raising critical concerns about the preservation of fundamental human-centered values, such as privacy, fairness, and safety. Although software engineering has traditionally focused on functional correctness, the adoption of LLMs and AI agents into complex socio-technical systems has intensified the need for responsible software engineering and robust value alignment. In MAS, architectural design decisions, such as coordination mechanisms, communication protocols, and system topologies, play a central role in shaping system behavior and the outcomes they produce. This paper argues that architectural choices influence not only the functionality and performance of MAS but can also promote value-oriented system behavior. Therefore, we investigate how different architectural designs support different human-centered values, discussing the following value-preserving architectural patterns: (i) a privacy-aware architecture with a federated topology, (ii) a distributed architecture to promote pluralism and diversity, and (iii) a guard-agent architecture to detect and mitigate unfairness. Finally, we introduce representative use cases to illustrate the proposed architectures in real-world scenarios. By linking architectural design with human-centered values, this work lays the foundation for a unified set of architectural patterns and guidelines towards the design of trustworthy MAS.

</details>


### [45] [Speak for Me: Giving LLMs the Situational Awareness to Participate in a Meeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03923)
*Muneeb Khan,Frederic Kirstein,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAPA(协作智能体预测架构)解决在线会议委派中LLM智能体无法识别发言时机的问题。通过Perceiver更新会议状态、Predictor预测对话走向、Controller决定是否发言及表达哪一命题、Generator按参会者风格措辞，并用两个评审器及校准器更新状态。在AMI语料上，CASR将静默率从51.4%降至2.5%，恢复度从26.1提升至52.2，幻觉率维持在0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线会议委派场景中，LLM智能体缺乏结构化方式来追踪立场、覆盖范围和发言权（floor），导致它们无法识别应当发言的时刻。仅靠提示词的委派智能体在AMI语料上对缺席参会者51.4%的发言机会保持沉默，存在严重的"识别缺口"（recognition gap）。

Method: 提出CAPA架构，包含五个模块：Perceiver从每一观察轮次更新会议状态；Predictor预测对话后续走向；Controller决定是否发言及提出哪个命题；Generator以参会者风格措辞生成发言；两个评审器(judges)对预测和行动对照下一观察轮进行打分；Recalibrator依据评审裁决更新会议状态用于未来决策。同时提出episode级评估协议，用受模式约束的LLM评审器判断代表在参会者实际观点单元周围"是否、何时、什么内容"的贡献。

Result: 在137场AMI会议中，CAPA将静默率从51.4%降低到2.5%，被认可的恢复度翻倍（26.1提升至52.2），幻觉率保持在0.6%。协议中受模式约束的LLM评审器与人类标注的一致性达到Cohen's kappa=0.71。失败模式从遗漏转为选择问题，每个残余的近失(near-miss)都可归因于架构特定模块。机制消融实验表明会议状态是弥合识别缺口的关键杠杆，而仅扩大原始上下文规模无法达到同样效果。

Conclusion: CAPA通过显式维护会议状态并预测对话走向，有效解决了LLM智能体在在线会议委派中的发言时机识别问题。验证了结构化状态追踪优于单纯扩大上下文的方法，并将主要挑战从"何时发言"（遗漏）转移到"说什么"（选择质量）。

Abstract: In online meeting delegation, LLM agents fail to recognize when to speak. With no structured way to track stances, coverage, and floor, they miss the moments where they should contribute. Prompt-only delegates stay silent on 51.4% of the absent participant's talking opportunities on the AMI corpus. We present CAPA (Collaborative Agent Predictive Architecture), an architecture for online meeting delegation. A Perceiver updates the meeting state from each observed turn. A Predictor forecasts how the conversation will continue. A Controller decides whether to speak and which proposition to surface. A Generator phrases the chosen contribution in the participant's style. Two judges score the forecast and the action against the next observed turn. A Recalibrator updates the meeting state from those verdicts for future decisions. To evaluate online delegation, we introduce an episode-level protocol that scores whether, when, and what a delegate contributes around the participant's actual idea units. The protocol's schema-constrained LLM judges align with human annotations at Cohen's kappa = 0.71. On 137 AMI meetings, CAPA reduces the silence rate from 51.4% to 2.5%, doubles credited recovery (26.1 --> 52.2), and keeps hallucination at 0.6%. The failure mode shifts from omission to selection, with each residual near-miss attributable to a specific module of the architecture. Mechanism ablations identify the meeting state as the lever that closes the recognition gap, where raw-context scaling alone does not.

</details>


### [46] [Towards Numerical TOHTN Planning with SMT-based HTN-SAT Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03938)
*Gaspard Quenard,Takudzwa Togarepi,Damien Pellier,Humbert Fiorino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了数值型全序HTN（TOHTN）规划问题，将标准SAT编码自然扩展为SMT以处理数值流（numeric fluents），并提出了该领域的首个基准测试套件。实验表明该简单编码已构成有竞争力的基线，为更富表达力的HTN规划方法开辟了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管HTN规划近年来受到广泛关注，但对数值推理（numerical reasoning）的支持仍然非常有限，现有方法缺乏处理数值流量的能力，且该领域缺少统一的基准测试用于评估。

Method: 作者将标准基于SAT的编码方式自然地扩展到SMT（可满足性模理论）框架中，以处理数值型全序HTN规划中的数值流。同时设计并引入了一套数值TOHTN规划的基准测试套件，为后续评估提供通用平台。

Result: 实验结果表明，这一简单直接的SMT编码已经能够构成一个有竞争力的基线方案，在该基准上表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了SMT扩展在数值TOHTN规划中的可行性和有效性，为未来开发更具表达力、更复杂的HTN规划方法打开了研究空间。

Abstract: While HTN planning has received significant attention in recent years, support for numerical reasoning remains very limited. In this paper, we investigate numerical Totally-Ordered HTN (TOHTN) planning and show how standard SAT-based encodings can be naturally extended with SMT to handle numeric fluents. In addition, we introduce a benchmark suite for numerical TOHTN planning, providing a first common basis for evaluation in this setting. Experimental results show that this simple encoding already constitutes a competitive baseline. This work opens the way to more expressive approaches to HTN planning.

</details>


### [47] [More Criticism Does Not Make a Better Review: EquiReview-R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03943)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yude Fu,Yang Kewei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI辅助评审重新定义为"基于证据引导的灵感集精炼"过程，将遗漏与过度批评视为两个独立风险。作者引入EquiReview-R系统，该系统根据局部证据解决现有问题，从独立和评审条件化角度搜索缺失问题，并返回停止、继续或推迟的决策。EquiReview-R在先前未见论文上减少了重大过度批评（15.5%降至8.1%），达到了9.9%的单侧遗漏上限，并在52.4%的论文上提前停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评审者能产生大量具体批评，但更多批评不等于更好评审。一个评审可能遗漏重要弱点，或保留缺乏证据支持的指控。前者需要补充，后者需要修正，但生成式系统和聚合指标掩盖了这种区分，亟需将遗漏与过度批评作为独立风险来对待。

Method: 1) 将AI辅助评审形式化为"基于证据引导的结构化关注点集合精炼"；2) 引入EquiReview-R系统，分三步操作：基于局部证据解决现有关注点、从独立与评审条件化视角搜索遗漏问题、输出停止/继续/推迟决策；3) 构建证据关联的轨迹语料库（ReviewTrace）用于暴露设计动机对应的失败模式；4) 在冻结的未见论文章上执行预注册的非劣效性评估。

Result: 在先前未见论文的冻结队列上：满足重大遗漏的预设非劣效性标准；将重大过度批评从15.5%降至8.1%；达到9.9%的单侧遗漏上限；在52.4%的论文上实现提前停止。计算匹配对照、受控配对和消融实验表明，增益来自修订过程而非额外推理或更短输出。

Conclusion: 修订必须先于进一步搜索：高召回率评审中几乎所有关注点缺乏明确的证据性判定，而早期精炼机制无法修订它们。EquiReview-R证明将遗漏与过度批评分离处理并优先进行基于证据的修订，是优于单纯生成更多批评的可比较且更可控的评审路径。作者发布ReviewTrace语料库以促进评审修订、分歧和溯源研究。

Abstract: AI reviewers can now produce many specific criticisms, but more criticism is not necessarily a better review. A review may miss a consequential weakness or retain an allegation that available evidence does not support. These failures require opposite corrections, yet generation-oriented systems and aggregate measures obscure the distinction. We therefore recast AI-assisted review as evidence-guided refinement of a structured concern set, with omission and overcritique treated as separate risks. Building on this formulation, we introduce EquiReview-R, which resolves existing concerns against localized evidence, searches for missing issues from independent and review-conditioned perspectives, and returns stop, continue, or defer. To expose the failure mode that motivates this design, we construct an evidence-linked trajectory corpus. Its retrospective analysis shows why revision must precede further search: nearly all concerns in a high-recall review lack a definitive evidential disposition, while an earlier refinement mechanism cannot revise them. On a frozen cohort of previously unseen papers, EquiReview-R satisfies the prespecified non-inferiority criterion for major omission, reduces major overcritique from 15.5% to 8.1%, and attains a one-sided omission upper bound of 9.9% while stopping on 52.4% of papers. Computation-matched controls, controlled pairs, and ablations show that the gain comes from revision rather than extra inference or shorter output. We release the corpus as ReviewTrace, an evidence-linked resource for studying review revision, disagreement, and provenance.

</details>

*NPCI AI Research Team,Aman Kumar,Asit Desai,Chandra Bhushan,Harsh Sharma,Harshit Bhushan,Hrithik Kadam,Keyur Doshi,Kolisetty Sai Kapardheeswar,Krishanu Adhikary,Nadeem Shaik,Navya Prakash,Nitin Kukreja,Prashant Devadiga,Shamanth MH,Shantanu Pandey,Suvradip Paul,Yatharth Dedhia*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建FiMI Banking测试环境，基于经过审查的银行文档、结构化地面真值、合成客户背景和银行工具。评估两种后训练方法：偏好优化（针对响应级行为）和带可验证奖励的强化学习（针对多轮工具使用任务）。

Result: 偏好优化将超出范围拒绝率从52%提升至80%；强化学习将边缘案例性能从0.509提升至0.718，顺序敏感任务性能从0.590提升至0.679，同时生成token减少29%。

Abstract: Banks need conversational systems that can answer product questions, assist customers with account-related requests, and operate safely within strict operational and regulatory constraints. General-purpose language models do not reliably meet these requirements. They fall short when a task requires grounded information, correct tool use, or cautious handling of bank-specific sensitive situations. We introduce FiMI Banking, a controlled Indian retail-banking setting. We build it from vetted banking documents, structured ground truth, synthetic customer backgrounds, and banking tools. We evaluate two post-training approaches: preference optimization for response-level behavior, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for multi-turn tool-use tasks. Preference optimization improves safe behavior substantially: out-of-scope refusal rises from 52% to 80%. Reinforcement learning improves edge-case performance from 0.509 to 0.718 and order-sensitive task performance from 0.590 to 0.679, while using 29% fewer generated tokens. These results show that preference optimization and verifiable-reward reinforcement learning address complementary requirements for reliable banking agents.

</details>


### [49] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个重大却发现：智能体评估中报告的工具调用率并非模型本身的性质，而是"模型-接口栈"的共同产物。通过在BFCL v4、tau-bench等多个基准上固定权重、算例、解码和随机种子，仅更换服务端适配器，同一模型的得分可在0.00与0.96/0.19之间剧烈波动。问题精确定位于聊天模板与解析器的交互效应（而非任一单组件缺陷），且该不匹配会渗透进训练循环产生规模依赖的后果。作者发布了一个98行预检脚本可捕捉所有此类静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理评估报告中显示的工具调用率可能严重失真——当模型确实发出格式良好的调用时，接口层可能在任何下游组件看到之前就审查/丢弃了轨迹，导致报告数字为零。作者旨在定位这一"静默失败"效应的精确来源、量化其规模依赖性，并提供诊断手段。

Method: 作者在BFCL v4原始数据、执行器和评分器上保持权重、算例、解码和种子固定，仅更换服务端适配器，进行2x2实验（聊天模板×解析器），精确定位效应存在于交互项而非主效应。在tau-bench的115个交互式零售任务上用相同替换对比服务器解析调用数与实际工具执行任务数。使用Qwen2.5-Coder进行21倍规模范围的探测，并以Llama-3.1-8B验证strict:true标志的影响。还检查了verl的AgentLoop训练循环中该问题的影响，并发布98行预检脚本。

Result: 2x2实验显示两个主效应均为零，全部效应位于交互项——修复合同单侧毫无收益。tau-bench上同一次替换使服务器解析调用从0增至636，实际执行工具的任务从0增至103。Qwen2.5-Coder在21倍规模范围内服务器解析始终0/100，而格式良好调用在32B时升至80/100（经裁定金标准校准后约72）。相反，在匹配包络下，静默部分保持0-2。Llama-3.1-8B的23%任务函数误调率在strict:true下归零。训练循环中，7B AgentLoop有45/115代完整调用但0被接受/执行/返回观测；评估阶段修复适配器后解析0->84、挽救0->9、通过率53->62（不显著）。

Conclusion: 观察到的工具调用率不是模型单独的性质，而是测量它的模型-接口栈的性质。不匹配源于聊天模板与解析器之间的交互合同缺陷。修复接口可以恢复机制（解析、执行均恢复正常），但不会带来显著的端到端任务性能提升，意味着接口层修复是必要非充分条件。98行预检脚本能捕获所有此类静默失败，建议作为评估前的标准检查。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [50] [Common-Witness Certificates and Sharp Feature Bounds for Counterfactual Image Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03973)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化地刻画了图像编辑中的"局部到全局失败"问题：一个编辑器可能分别满足每个区域合理性约束，但没有任何单一潜在解释能适配完整输出。作者提出使用公共证人评级（common witness grade）和证人神经（witness nerve）框架，将审计与因果识别分离，并给出基于Helly类论证的短不兼容性证书、阻塞超图谱公式的精确修复计数，以及针对区域边缘分布的同步置信区域，实现有限样本的完整识别区间外覆盖。实验（含MNIST、Morpho-MNIST、smallNORB和合成数据）验证了预测的局部-全局分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑模型可能对每个局部区域都满足合理性约束，但这些局部满足无法保证整体输出存在一致的潜在解释——即局部达到全局失败的困境。现有方法缺乏对这一系统性失败的形式化框架，也未能区分审计（检测局部-全局不一致）与因果识别（对预设图像特征的推断）这两个不同任务。

Method: 提出公共证人评级和证人神经作为形式化工具来描述局部-全局分离；框架分离审计与因果识别两条路径——共享外生性仅允许区域边缘分布的任意耦合，外部证实的证人类系则可对预设图像特征产生尖锐的部分识别界。利用Helly类论证为拟凸损失、异构作用层和有限证人图册提供短不兼容性证书；提出阻塞超图谱公式计算精确修复次数；构造区域边缘分布的同步置信区域以给出有限样本外覆盖。

Result: 在受控MNIST、Morpho-MNIST和smallNORB实验上验证了预测的局部-全局分离现象；合成实验测试了尖锐界的有效性、证书的恢复能力及结构化计算性能。方法成功实现局部合理性审计与全局不一致的量化检测。

Conclusion: 该方法专注于审计声明的特征关系，而非识别无限制的像素级反事实。框架为图像编辑的局部-全局合理性问题提供了可计算的形式化审计工具，明确了审计与因果识别的边界和条件。

Abstract: An image editor may satisfy every regional plausibility constraint separately even when no single latent explanation fits the complete output. We formalize this local-to-global failure using a common witness grade and witness nerve. The framework separates auditing from causal identification: shared exogeneity alone allows every coupling of the regime marginals, whereas an externally justified witness relation yields sharp partial-identification bounds for prespecified image features. Helly-type arguments provide short incompatibility certificates for quasiconvex losses, heterogeneous action strata, and finite witness atlases; a blocker-hypergraph formula gives exact repair counts. Simultaneous confidence regions for the regime marginals give finite-sample outer coverage of the complete identified interval. Controlled MNIST, Morpho-MNIST, and smallNORB studies demonstrate the predicted local-global separation, while synthetic experiments test sharp bounds, certificate recovery, and structured computation. The method audits a declared feature relation and does not identify unrestricted pixel-level counterfactuals.

</details>


### [51] [The Dually Flat Geometry of Planning as Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04005)
*Nikola Milosevic,Asaki Kataoka,Nicolas Hinrichs,Kenji Doya,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过重置规划过程将规划准则嵌入动力学中，提出占用度量的另一种刻画——访问度量，并证明可达访问度量构成一个双平坦统计流形。其两个仿射坐标分别为访问概率与对数策略，二者在条件熵下对偶，使规划即推理从线性奖励推广到访问的非线性泛函，每步迭代由一次自然梯度更新求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的占用度量虽常用于规划即推理框架，但其内在的几何结构尚未被充分揭示。作者希望找到一个更自然的数学对象与几何框架，用以表达决策过程中的信息几何，从而统一并推广规划即推理方法，使其适用于非线性奖励泛函。

Method: 将规划准则嵌入MDP动力学中，通过重置规划过程构造一个新的平稳度量（访问度量）；在此基础上建立可达访问度量集合作为双平坦统计流形，其两个对偶仿射坐标系分别是访问概率分布和对数策略，对偶函数为条件熵；利用自然梯度步迭代求解规划问题，并将时序差分误差解释为边际效用的估计量。

Result: 证明访问度量构成的流形具有双平坦几何结构；规划即推理从线性奖励情形推广到访问度量的非线性泛函；每次迭代对应一个自然梯度步骤；给出了时序差分误差在边际效用意义上的统计解释，并探讨了该几何对强化学习与理论神经科学的意义。

Conclusion: 访问度量及其双平坦几何为强化学习中的规划即推理提供了统一的数学框架，使奖励泛函、梯度更新与TD误差均获得一致的几何信息论解释，并可为理论神经科学中的相关机制提供启示。

Abstract: We present an alternative characterization of the occupancy measure of reinforcement learning, obtained by embedding the planning criterion into the dynamics through a resetting planning process. Its stationary measure, which we term visitation measure, is the object on which the information geometry of decision making is most naturally expressed. The achievable visitation measures form a dually flat statistical manifold whose two affine charts are the visitation probabilities and the log-policies, dual under the conditional entropy. This structure makes planning-as-inference generalize from linear rewards to nonlinear functionals of the visitation, each iterate solved by one natural-gradient step, and gives the temporal-difference error the interpretation of a marginal-utility estimate. We develop the geometry and its consequences for reinforcement learning and theoretical neuroscience.

</details>


### [52] [LLM4CKD: Large Language Models for Early Stage Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04013)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLMs）在零样本和少样本情境学习下用于慢性肾病（CKD）筛查的有效性，并与传统机器学习和深度学习方法进行比较。结果表明LLMs在低数据场景下能以少量示例取得有竞争力的性能，但稳定性不如传统模型；研究提出LLM可作为标注数据有限时CKD筛查的灵活补充工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病（CKD）的早期筛查对及时干预至关重要，但大多数机器学习和深度学习方法依赖标注数据与模型训练，限制了它们在真实筛查场景中的应用。作者希望探索无需任务特定训练的大语言模型是否能通过上下文学习实现CKD筛查。

Method: 提出一个框架，使用临床选择的表格特征和结构化提示模板，使LLM在无需任务特定训练的情况下进行推断。LLM在多种提示风格、特征配置和数据设置下进行评估，并与标准机器学习、深度学习、表格基础模型（TFM）基线以及现有CKD筛查工具进行比较。

Result: LLMs仅使用少量示例就能取得有竞争力的性能，在低数据场景下常能匹配甚至超越传统方法。但LLM的性能依赖具体模型选择，且随输入复杂度增加而稳定性下降。相反，ML、DL和TFM模型在大规模训练数据下表现出更一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: LLMs与传统方法在数据效率和稳定性之间存在权衡。LLMs可以作为标注数据有限时CKD筛查的灵活补充性工具，而非替代传统方法。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is critical for timely intervention, yet most machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches require labeled data and model training, limiting their use in real-world screening settings. This study evaluates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) for CKD screening under zero-shot and few-shot in-context learning settings and compares them with traditional ML and DL methods. We propose a framework that uses clinically selected tabular features and structured prompt templates to enable LLM-based inference without task-specific training. LLM performance is evaluated across multiple prompt styles, feature configurations, and data settings, and compared with standard ML, DL, and tabular foundation model (TFM) baselines, and existing CKD screening tools. The results show that LLMs can achieve competitive performance using only a small number of examples, often matching or outperforming traditional approaches in low-data settings. However, their performance remains model-dependent and less stable as input complexity increases. In contrast, ML, DL, and TFM models show more consistent improvement with larger training data. Overall, the findings highlight a trade-off between data efficiency and stability, suggesting that LLMs may serve as a flexible complementary approach for CKD screening when labeled data are limited.

</details>


### [53] [InSituMeasure: Probing Situated Measurement Grounding in Industrial Scenes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04014)
*Chao Shen,Xinyuan Li,Yunfan Zhou,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Zhihai Wang,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSituMeasure基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在真实工业场景中进行表计读数的情境化测量能力。该基准包含2,922个真实工业监控场景、8类专业工程仪表，配有密集的表计属性标注和噪声标签。在24个先进MLLMs上的评估显示，最佳模型仅达到25.7%的数值-单位联合准确率和51.8%的置信度诊断F1分数，揭示出通用多模态能力与可靠情境化测量之间存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在通用多模态基准上表现强劲，但在连续值测量任务（如表计读数）上仍不可靠。已有基准虽然暴露了这一弱点，但将测量从现实、知识引导的场景中隔离出来，缺乏情境上下文、专业仪器、真实噪声和匹配的诊断标注，导致真实感不足且无法进行根因分析。因此亟需一个更接近真实情境的基准来系统评估和诊断模型的测量能力。

Method: 作者构建了InSituMeasure基准，包含2,922个真实工业监控场景，涵盖8个功能类别的专业工程仪表，并提供了密集的表计属性标注和噪声标签。同时定义了多维度评估指标：数值精度（预定义容差下）与单位一致性、对虚假或不可回答问题（假任务）的拒答能力，以及模型失败与标注误差因素之间的对齐程度。作者在24个先进的MLLMs上进行了系统评估。

Result: 在24个先进的MLLMs中，最佳模型仅达到25.7%的联合数值-单位准确率和51.8%的置信度诊断F1分数。进一步分析识别出了由文本引导的捷径、过度自信响应以及真实工业噪声（包括混合干扰、视角偏离、遮挡和环境干扰）导致的失败模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示出通用多模态能力与可靠的情境化测量之间存在实质性差距，表明当前MLLMs尚不足以胜任真实工业场景中需要专业知识和连续值精确判读的测量任务。该基准为未来提升MLLMs在实际测量场景中的可靠性和诊断能力提供了评估工具和改进方向。

Abstract: For trained operators, gauge reading requires little specialized knowledge, low cognitive effort, and high repeatability. Yet Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable in continuous-valued measurement despite strong results on general multimodal benchmarks. Existing benchmarks expose this weakness but isolate measurement from realistic, knowledge-grounded settings, with limited situated context, specialized instruments, real-world noise, and matched diagnostic annotations, reducing realism and constraining root-cause analysis. We introduce InSituMeasure to evaluate situated measurement grounding. It contains 2,922 real industrial monitoring scenes across eight functional categories of professional engineering instruments, with dense gauge-attribute annotations and noise tags for failure diagnosis. We define metrics for numerical accuracy under predefined tolerances and unit consistency, rejection of fake or unanswerable tasks, and alignment between model failures and annotated error factors. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, the best model reaches only 25.7\% joint value-unit accuracy and 51.8\% confidence-diagnosis F1, revealing a substantial gap between general multimodal competence and reliable situated measurement. Further analysis identifies failures from text-induced shortcuts, overconfident responses, and authentic industrial noise, including mixed disturbances, viewpoint deviation, occlusion, and environmental interference.

</details>


### [54] [FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04021)
*Yalun Wu,Junfeng Fang,Jiawei Wang,Haotian Liu,Qijun Yang,Minghan Yang,Hongcheng Guo,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FLY-EVAL++，一个面向安全关键、物理约束环境的证据驱动型LLM评估协议，结合确定性验证与固定评分规则，在飞行轨迹与姿态预测任务上对66个LLM进行评估，发现安全合规是最具区分度的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于准确率的评估指标无法可靠检测LLM在安全关键、物理约束环境中的关键失效模式，如违规操作约束、物理不一致的组合预测、无法输出可用结构化结果等。需要更可靠的评估协议。

Method: 提出FLY-EVAL++评估协议，包含两部分：(1) 对协议合规性、物理可行性、安全约束进行确定性验证；(2) 固定评分规则引导的多维度分数聚合。在PilotBench基础上扩展历史条件预测和多步预测任务，实例化为飞行轨迹与姿态预测(FTAP)评估。

Result: 在66个LLM上，安全合规是最具区分力的行为维度：预测性能相近的模型在安全分数上差异超过28个点；观察到反复出现的失效模式，包括物理上看似合理但违反安全约束的预测，以及多步滚动预测中的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 安全关键领域的评估应显式测量约束满足和结构化有效性，而不能仅依赖以准确率为中心的报告。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.

</details>


### [55] [Instruction Duplication as an Inference-Time Control Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04024)
*Victor Lavrenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令重复"（instruction duplication）这一极简黑盒推理期控制方法：将过程性指令重复两遍即可在不重训、不改解码的情况下提升模型遵循指令的可靠性。在7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种放置条件和16800次调度生成中，将全八项诊断测试通过率从90.22%提升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除了一拷贝后30.2%的残余失败。但盲审挑战未达到预设的28/30确认标准（仅10/30方向性确认）。其操作价值取决于下游系统的消费方式：在Answer Engineering（显式轨迹状态决定局部修复）中配合使用时可将端到端归因提升至97.1%（相对于无编辑发布的25.1%基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程性指令遵循是可控语言模型系统的基础要求，尤其在生成的轨迹需要被下游检查或修复时。作者希望探索一种不改变训练与解码过程的最小干预手段来提升模型的指令遵循能力。

Method: 指令重复是一种纯推理期黑盒控制：仅将过程性指令文本重复一次（从一拷贝增至两拷贝），不进行任何重训练或解码修改。实验覆盖7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种指令放置条件、共16800次调度生成，并采用All-8诊断、TF-IDF召回、最终答案准确率及过早承诺等指标评估，同时配合盲审挑战和Answer Engineering下游系统测试。

Result: 将指令从1份增至2份时，确定性All-8诊断通过率从90.22%提升到93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除了1份后30.2%的残余失败。预审TF-IDF召回从73.44%升至74.81%（Holm校正p<.001），最终答案准确率保持精确的60.21%不变。过早承诺从1.52%升至2.30%（p_Holm=.00536）。盲审挑战仅获得10/30方向性确认和20/30感知平手，无逆转，未达预设28/30确认标准。在Answer Engineering中：SSNHL端点的无编辑发布基线为25.1%，复现的系统级AE为84.2%，加尾随重复后升至97.1%；但传导性诊断分支保存在AE中表现为下降（从78.6%降至73.8%），仍高于无编辑基线58.9%达14.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 指令重复是一种低复杂度、对放置位置敏感的推理期控制手段，其优势与劣势不能仅凭离线指标判断，而需要通过消费暴露轨迹的下游系统来体现实际价值。虽然独立盲审未能确认其感知层面的显著优势，但在Answer Engineering这类依赖显式轨迹状态做局部修复的系统中，配合使用可带来实质性收益，说明此类控制的实用价值是下游系统相关的（system-contingent）。

Abstract: Procedural instruction following is a basic requirement for controllable language-model systems, especially when generated trajectories are inspected or repaired downstream. We introduce instruction duplication, a minimal black-box inference-time control that repeats only the procedural instruction, without retraining or decoding changes. Across seven instruction-tuned models, 300 medical multiple-choice questions, eight placement conditions, and 16,800 scheduled generations, moving from one to two copies raises the deterministic All-8 diagnostic--responses passing all eight observable tests--from 90.22% to 93.17% (+2.95 percentage points), eliminating 30.2% of the failures remaining after one copy. Pre-provisional TF-IDF recall rises from 73.44% to 74.81% (+1.38 points; Holm-adjusted p < .001), while final-answer accuracy remains exactly 60.21%. Premature commitment increases from 1.52% to 2.30% (p_Holm = .00536). A blinded challenge audit yields 10/30 directional confirmations, 20/30 perceptual ties, and no reversals; its prespecified 28/30 confirmation criterion is not met. Yet this distinction can matter operationally when a downstream system acts on the generated trajectory. In Answer Engineering (AE), where explicit trajectory state determines local repair, the published reason-first no-editing SSNHL endpoint was 25.1%; system-only AE was later reproduced at 84.2%, and the same trailing duplicate raised it to 97.1%. For conductive diagnostic branch preservation, the corresponding values are 58.9% published without editing, 78.6% with reproduced AE, and 73.8% with AE plus duplication--a within-AE decrease, but still 14.9 points above the no-editing baseline. Instruction duplication is therefore a low-complexity, placement-sensitive control whose practical value can emerge through the downstream system that consumes the exposed trajectory.

</details>


### [56] [IRWOZ 2.0: A Large Language Model-driven Dialogue Dataset for Industrial Robot Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04030)
*Chen Li,Dimitrios Chrysostomou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IRWOZ 2.0，一个通过大语言模型增强生成和质量精炼而改进的工业人机交互对话数据集。该数据集扩展至390个对话，覆盖4个工业领域，并通过对话状态跟踪基准实验验证了显著性能提升（GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从0.1651提升至0.5604），数据集已公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始IRWOZ数据集虽然通过领域特定标注改进了工业人机交互对话系统，但其初始版本在对话状态和话语中包含大量噪声，限制了状态跟踪的准确性，因此需要改进版本以解决这些局限。

Method: 利用大语言模型（Mistral/Claude-3.5）增强对话生成并进行质量精炼，包括人工修正和自动化错别字移除，扩展数据集至390个对话并覆盖4个工业领域（Assembly、Delivery、Position、Relocation）。

Result: 在对话状态跟踪基准实验中，与原始IRWOZ相比取得了显著改进，GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从0.1651提升至0.5604。

Conclusion: IRWOZ 2.0通过LLM驱动的生成和质量优化有效解决了原始数据集噪声问题，显著提升了工业HRI对话状态跟踪的性能，并已公开数据集以支持工业人机交互研究。

Abstract: IRWOZ has improved industrial human-robot interaction (HRI) dialogue systems through domain-specific annotations. However, its initial version contains substantial noise in dialogue states and utterances, limiting state-tracking accuracy. We introduce IRWOZ 2.0, which addresses these limitations through large language model (LLM) enhanced generation (Mistral/Claude-3.5) and quality refinements. Our improved dataset expands to 390 dialogues across 4 industrial domains (Assembly, Delivery, Position, Relocation), featuring manual corrections and automated typo removal. Benchmark experiments on dialogue state tracking demonstrate significant improvements, with GPT-2's BLEU-4 score increasing from 0.1651 to 0.5604 compared to original IRWOZ. To support industrial HRI research, we publicly released IRWOZ 2.0 dataset at https://ieee-dataport.org/documents/irwoz-20-large-language-model-driven-dialogue-dataset-industrial-robot-conversations

</details>


### [57] [Spurious Advantage Hidden in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04063)
*Jiamian Wang,Samyadeep Basu,Koustava Goswami,Tong Yu,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现GRPO方法在可验证奖励的强化学习中存在"虚假优势"（spurious advantage）问题：某些通过猜测或搜索路径到达正确答案的rollout会被奖励统计赋予高优势值，误导策略朝猜测式行为发展。作者提出SIGNBALANCE，一种无组合性的优势估计方法，保留验证器符号、使用全局尺度，并通过stop-gradient的按类缩放恢复零均值平衡。实验表明该方法在边界答案数学和搜索智能体任务上优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO的优势估计器根据组内奖励统计赋予每个rollout一个幅度，但它在给"通过推理得出正确答案"的rollout高奖励的同时，也会给"通过猜测或预算作弊得到正确答案"的rollout分配同样的高幅度，即虚假优势。这种虚假优势会误导策略朝猜测式行为发展，而现有方法未区分这两种表面相同但本质不同的情况。

Method: 提出SIGNBALANCE：其幅度是"组合无关"（composition-free）的，即不再将优势分解为组内相对统计量。具体而言，它保留验证器给出的符号（sign），使用全局尺度（global scale），并通过带stop-gradient的按类别缩放（per-class rescaling）来恢复零均值平衡，从而避免放大虚假优势。

Result: 在数学和搜索智能体基准上、不同规模下进行实验，SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学任务上与GRPO持平，在边界答案数学任务和搜索智能体上优于GRPO。

Conclusion: GRPO等基于组内统计的优势估计存在忽视猜测式rollout的虚假优势缺陷，而SIGNBALANCE通过设计组合无关的幅度能有效消除该问题，在猜测易发的场景（小候选集、边界子题、多路径搜索）中获得更优性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely studied for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where its advantage estimator assigns each rollout a magnitude from within-group reward statistics. In the common case, this magnitude rewards rollouts that reach the correct answer through reasoning. Yet, an overlooked case shares the same surface: a rollout may land on it by guessing, and the formula still assigns a high magnitude, which we identify as the spurious advantage. This arises in three cases: bounded-answer tasks with a small candidate set; open-answer sets hosting bounded sub-cases; and search agents whose budget opens many paths to the same answer. In all three, this misleads the policy toward guess-like behaviors. We propose SIGNBALANCE, whose magnitude is composition-free: it keeps the verifier sign, uses a global scale, and restores zero-mean balance via a stop-gradient per-class rescaling. Across math and search agent benchmarks at different scales, SIGNBALANCE matches GRPO on open-answer math and improves on bounded-answer math and search agents. Code will be released.

</details>


### [58] [DRACO: Fine-Grained Credit Assignment with Dynamic Rubrics for Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04094)
*Shubham Gandhi,Saurabh Goyal,Kiran Kate,Yara Rizk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRACO框架，解决没有程序化验证器的长时程智能体环境中强化学习奖励稀疏的问题。通过动态生成多准则评分标准（rubrics）、对完整轨迹进行单次评分、并将评分以闭式方式重新分配给对应的分步步骤，从而在GRPO中产生细粒度的分步优势信号。在AppWorld上比基础模型提升15.9分，比使用稀疏真实奖励训练的GRPO提升5.3分；在域外Tau-Bench上无需前沿审判模型也能取得5.3分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在存在程序化检查器的任务中表现良好，但大多数长时程智能体环境缺乏此类检查器。在outcome-blind设置中，地面真值成功信号不可得。多准则评分标准虽能提供奖励，但每条轨迹只打一个标量分数，无法为数十个步骤提供细粒度的学习信号，导致信用分配困难。

Method: DRACO包含三个核心步骤：1) 在训练过程中动态生成评分标准（rubrics），以跟踪策略不断演进的能力；2) 每个完成的轨迹只对这些rubrics评分一次；3) 依据哪些步骤负责哪些被标注的rubrics，将评分以闭式方式重新分配给相应步骤，在GRPO中产生差异化的逐步骤优势值。该再分配为闭式解析形式，不引入任何可训练的归因模块。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上，DRACO相对基础模型提升15.9分，相对使用稀疏地面真值奖励训练的GRPO提升5.3分，且完全不需要使用任何验证器。在域外Tau-Bench基准上，即使没有前沿judge模型，DRACO相对基础模型依然提升5.3分，优于地面真值奖励训练和其他基于rubric的训练设置。

Conclusion: DRACO有效缓解了长时程agent域中奖励稀疏与信用分配问题，通过动态rubric加闭式优势再分配，在不依赖程序化验证器或额外训练归因模块的情况下取得优于甚至超越稀疏地面真值奖励训练的性能，展现了良好的泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards works well when a task has a programmatic checker, but most long-horizon agent domains have none. We work in the outcome-blind setting, where ground-truth success signals are not available. Multi-criteria rubrics are a popular way to supply such a reward; they are scored once per trajectory, but a single scalar is a poor signal across tens of steps. We propose DRACO: Distributing Rubric-based Advantage for Credit Optimization. It generates rubrics dynamically during training to track the policy's evolving capability, scores those rubrics once per completed trajectory, and redistributes that judgment over the steps responsible for annotated rubrics to produce differentiated per-step advantages in GRPO. The redistribution is closed-form and does not introduce any trained attribution module. On AppWorld, DRACO gains 15.9 points over the base model and 5.3 points over GRPO trained with a sparse ground-truth reward, despite not using any verifiers itself. On out-of-domain Tau-Bench, it gains 5.3 points over the base model even without a frontier judge, beating both ground-truth-reward training and other rubric-based training settings. The code for DRACO is available at https://github.com/IBM/draco.

</details>


### [59] [Why Gated DeltaNet Survives 4-Bit Quantization: NVFP4 W4A4 for the Recurrent Half of a Hybrid 27B LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04098)
*Sergii Kozyrev,Davyd Maiboroda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Minima，对Qwen3.8-27B混合LLM（含GDN线性注意力层）的全部496个线性层进行NVFP4 W4A4量化，实现BF16等同精度（5任务平均仅-0.52），同时模型最小（17.5 GiB）且prefill最快（+14-19%）。通过四部分机制研究，作者解释了为何混合LLM的循环部分（GDN层）反而是最容易量化的部分：NVFP4块级缩放抑制离群值、门控投影参数化压缩误差、delta规则平滑噪声不累积、每token量化代价随上下文被稀释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区早期对Qwen3.8-27B的4-bit量化通常将含衰减和写强度门控的GDN层保留在更高精度，直觉认为循环状态中误差会在长上下文中累积。作者希望检验这一假设，探究混合LLM中线性注意力层是否真的"难以量化"。

Method: 构建Minima：在Qwen3.8-27B上对所有496个线性层（含GDN层）采用NVFP4 W4A4量化。采用16元素块缩放（NVFP4）以定位残差流的极端离群值；通过软加/exponential与sigmoid参数化的门控投影压缩误差；修复per-module校准的NVFP4 checkpoint被融合kernel服务时产生的全局尺度不匹配；同时共享校准的FP8 KV-cache尺度。评估涵盖perplexity（4K/32K）、多项基准（MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench、RULER到64K）。

Result: Minima在所有下游基准上匹配BF16（在种子噪声内），5任务平均差异仅-0.52；8-bit GDN基线差6倍。32K perplexity差距随位置缩短。它是所比较方案中最小（17.5 GiB）且prefill最快（+14-19%）的。机制研究表明：门控投影最不敏感（~11% GEMM误差压缩到~2%输出误差）；delta-rule循环在32K token上噪声保持平坦平台并数百步内遗忘状态脉冲；per-token量化成本随上下文被稀释而不会累积。

Conclusion: 本文给出实用量化工序：量化一切、携带KV尺度——并提供了机制性解释，说明混合LLM的循环半部分是更容易量化的部分，推翻了一线社区中"循环层需保留高精度"的直觉。公布的checkpoint已在HuggingFace开源。

Abstract: Hybrid LLMs pair softmax attention with linear-attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), whose recurrent state summarizes the context in fixed size. Early community 4-bit quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B (48 GDN layers, 16 attention layers) left the GDN block in 8- or 16-bit precision -- especially its decay and write-strength gates -- on the intuition that errors in a recurrence accumulate over long contexts. We test that intuition by building Minima: NVFP4 W4A4 on all 496 linear layers, GDN included. Across perplexity at 4K/32K, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K, AIME'25, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench, and RULER retrieval to 64K, Minima matches BF16 within seed noise (5-task average -0.52) while being the smallest (17.5 GiB) and fastest-prefill (+14-19%) recipe we compare, and its 32K perplexity gap shrinks with position. A four-part mechanism study explains why: (i) NVFP4's 16-element block scaling localizes the residual stream's extreme outliers, equalizing activation error across layer roles; (ii) the supposedly fragile gate projections are the least sensitive -- softplus/exponential and sigmoid parameterizations compress ~11% GEMM error to ~2% output error; (iii) the delta-rule recurrence holds injected noise at a flat plateau over 32K tokens and forgets a state impulse within hundreds of steps, because each write overwrites the state along the current key direction; (iv) the per-token quantization cost washes out with context instead of compounding. We also repair a global-scale mismatch that arises when per-module-calibrated NVFP4 checkpoints are served by kernels that fuse those modules into one GEMM, and show calibrated FP8 KV-cache scales are performance-free. The result: a practical recipe -- quantize everything, ship KV scales -- and a mechanistic account of why the recurrent half of a hybrid LLM is the easy half to quantize. Checkpoint: https://huggingface.co/minima-ai/mnma_qwen3.8_27b_nvfp4

</details>


### [60] [Epistemic Warrant for LLM Recommendations: Characterizing the Basis for Reliance When Ground Truth Is Unavailable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04127)
*Shai Vardi,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入"认知依据"（epistemic warrant）这一决策层面的构念，用于刻画大型语言模型（LLM）在缺乏客观真值条件下单个成对推荐的依据强度，并通过四层倚赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）加以操作化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于支持组织决策，但用户缺乏原则性依据来判断是否应依赖某个具体推荐。现有方法通常评估模型整体属性（如可靠性、不确定性、稳健性）或关注用户信任，而非单个推荐背后的依据基础。

Method: 从认识论（epistemology）引入理论根基，提出"认知依据"构念，刻画模型偏好的稳定性及该偏好成立的范围；并将其操作化为四层倚赖证书，区分不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持和广泛支持的成对推荐。采用已知组检验（known-groups tests）验证专家预设的依序排序，并与众包工人的独立共识进行系统性对照。

Result: 已知组检验成功恢复了专家预设的依据排序；更强的依据与独立众包共识系统性一致；认知依据提供了区别于语言化置信度（verbalized confidence）的独立信息，且不能简单由决策难度来解释。

Conclusion: 该框架为在客观真值不可得时刻画单个LLM推荐依据提供了有理论根基、可实施的方案，助力用户判断何时应倚赖某一具体推荐。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to support organizational decisions, yet users often lack a principled basis for assessing whether to rely on a specific recommendation. Existing approaches typically evaluate broad model properties, such as reliability, uncertainty, or robustness, or focus on user trust, rather than the underlying basis for relying on an individual recommendation. Adapting theoretical foundations from epistemology, we introduce epistemic warrant, a decision-level construct that characterizes the stability of a model's preference and the scope over which that preference holds. We operationalize this construct through a four-tier reliance certificate for pairwise recommendations, distinguishing among unstable, context-dependent, locally supported, and broadly supported recommendations. We validate the construct using contemporary methodologies: known-groups tests successfully recover expert-prespecified warrant orderings, and stronger warrants systematically align with independent consensus from crowd workers. Furthermore, we demonstrate that epistemic warrant provides information distinct from verbalized confidence and is not readily explained by decision difficulty. Ultimately, this framework offers a theoretically grounded, implementable approach for characterizing the warrant of individual LLM recommendations when objective ground truth is unavailable.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿模型能力增强，从零合成(scaffolding from scratch)的环境变得越来越不具有挑战性，提供的学习信号有限。现有协同进化(co-evolution)方法虽能迭代合成接近模型可学习边界的环境，但其依赖on-policy回滚，限制了泛化能力，且随着模型变强难以持续提供学习信号，因此需要一种离策略且可持续调度的环境生成方法。

Result: 在Hy4 preview、Claude Opus 5和GPT-5.6 Sol上的量化回滚实验表明，环境进化持续产生更难的环境；在Qwen3.6-27B和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上通过简单的长时程RL训练，分别在Terminal-Bench 2.1上提升了14.4和18.0个百分点的性能。

Conclusion: 环境进化方法通过离策略的难度递增与按代调度，解决了协同进化方法中on-policy回滚带来的泛化局限与学习信号不可持续的问题，能够持续为终端智能体训练提供有效学习信号并显著提升下游性能。

Abstract: Scaling interactive and verifiable environments is critical for training terminal agents. As frontier models become more capable, environments synthesized from scratch become less challenging and thus provide limited learning signals. Recent co-evolution methods iteratively synthesize environments near the model's learnable frontier based on weaknesses exposed during rollouts. However, their dependence on on-policy rollouts limits generalization and the continuous provision of learning signals as the model becomes stronger. In this paper, we propose environment evolution, which incrementally increases environment difficulty off-policy and schedules the evolved environments generation by generation during training to provide continuous learning signals. We derive three evolution directions that influence environment difficulty from the multi-turn learning objective and then implement evolution along these directions through a loop-engineered multi-agent harness. Quantitative rollout experiments with Hy4 preview, Claude Opus 5, and GPT-5.6 Sol show that environment evolution consistently produces more difficult environments. We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [62] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理正越来越多地跨组织部署，但使用异构的开发框架、AI模型、工具接口、协议和执行环境。为了发挥其社会和商业影响潜力，这些代理必须通过统一的通信协议实现互操作。

Method: NLIP作为应用层协议，提供轻量级语义消息信封，可通过HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket和AMQP等现有传输层承载。协议允许支持NLIP的代理和网关在客户端、代理、本地上下文存储、本体、工具、企业服务及异构底层协议之间进行适配，并强调安全设计原则及参考实现。

Result: 论文呈现了NLIP的动机与设计原理、消息模型与传输绑定、安全设计考量、参考实现、代表性应用、采用信号，以及与MCP和A2A等新兴代理协议的关系。

Conclusion: NLIP通过标准化应用层协议解决了异构AI代理间的互操作挑战，为跨组织AI代理生态系统的协同提供了基础通信框架。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [63] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时自适应（TAHI），利用跨会话的人机交互数据将个体用户标准融入AI代理的上下文和权重中，通过演化的评分标准模块将用户训练与评估标准显式化，从而弥合AI从大众能力到个体专家水准的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理虽在大规模数据上训练并获得广泛能力，但其产出难以达到专业人士愿意为之背书的高标准。在开放、现实的任务中，成功标准是异构且未充分记录的，个体专长恰恰体现在超越平均水平的提升上。用户无法预先完整阐明标准，却会在多次任务中反复应用这些隐性标准，这构成了未被充分利用的个性化信号。

Method: 提出TAHI（test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction），将跨会话的交互信号整合进代理的上下文与权重中，并通过一个演化的评分标准模块显式固化每位用户的训练与评估标准。在写作与视觉创作两个高实用领域中对30位个体、共600个任务进行代理自适应。

Result: 在数十个任务内，代理单任务成功率提升4.5-20.9%；演化的评分标准模块作为可扩展标注工具，相比仅由大语言模型或人类创建的标准能多捕捉16.0-22.3%的失败。此外，面向个体的个性化代理也产生了最高8.8%的跨用户泛化成功提升。

Conclusion: 跨会话的人机交互数据是弥合AI到个体专长差距的关键信号。TAHI证明通过整合这些信号可实现个体化适配，同时演化评分标准既服务个性化又能产生一定的跨用户泛化收益，为AI从通用能力走向个人专业化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [64] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Terminal-Universe框架，将已有的终端智能体轨迹重构为可复用的、可执行的环境，并从中合成新的训练任务，通过广度（跨代码库查询）和深度（多轮交互会话）两个维度扩展任务集，显著提升了智能体在单轮和多轮基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端型代码智能体轨迹已大量积累，但真实、可执行的环境仍然稀缺。后训练阶段需要的是可执行环境（可重复查询生成多种可验证任务并提供执行反馈），而非一次性冻结的演示轨迹。从零生成环境困难，而利用现有轨迹中暴露的环境结构来重建环境是一条更可行的路径。

Method: Terminal-Universe框架包含三步：(1) 回放轨迹中记录的文件操作，恢复智能体修改前各文件的状态，得到部分工作区；由补全智能体补充缺失文件和依赖；(2) 在恢复的工作区上重建原始意图任务并合成新任务；(3) 沿两个轴扩展：广度上挖掘相关环境间的方向性依赖关系，合成跨代码库查询；深度上通过用户智能体将单轮查询扩展为多轮会话，捕捉迭代式用户反馈和需求细化。

Result: 在公开的终端智能体轨迹上应用该方法，生成了37.3k个任务充分的环境。对Qwen3.5-27B进行监督微调后，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上单轮性能提升11.9分，在EvoCode-Bench v2上的多轮性能MT@4提升13.8分。

Conclusion: 轨迹本身包含的环境结构信息足以重建可执行环境，从而将冻结的演示数据转化为可复用的训练环境库；通过合成任务的广度与深度双向扩展，能够有效提升终端智能体在单轮与多轮场景下的能力。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [65] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入了一套因果分类框架，用于区分语言模型的"欺骗性行为"与"欺骗性机制"，通过受控实验证明表观欺骗行为可能在没有对应机制的情况下产生，同时也证明了接收者信息状态能因果影响欺骗偏好，但即便存在欺骗机制证据也不能确立模型在欺骗中的自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究界和新闻界在讨论语言模型欺骗时，越来越多地将类人心智状态概念归于模型，这模糊了"行为看起来像欺骗"与"机制实际上在欺骗"之间的界限。作者希望通过因果分类学来澄清这些概念混淆。

Method: 作者引入了包含多个维度区分的因果分类体系：先验承诺与回顾性报告、模型偏好与已实现输出、虚假偏好与对误导接收者效用的敏感性、欺骗行为与产生该行为的目标或策略的来源。并在两个开放权重模型家族上进行受控猜谜游戏和股票交易实验加以检验。

Result: 实验结果发现：表观欺骗行为可以在没有相应欺骗机制的情况下产生；同时部分干预实验直接证明接收者的信息状态能因果影响欺骗偏好。这表明欺骗行为可以为欺骗机制提供证据，但即便存在机制证据，也不能据此确立模型在欺骗中的主体性。

Conclusion: 作者得出的核心结论是：欺骗性行为能够作为欺骗机制存在的证据，但机制层面的证据并不等同于模型具有欺骗的自主性/能动性，两者需要严格区分对待。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [66] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个由100个自主LLM智能体组成的科研集体（任务为证明形式化数学猜想）进行案例研究，观察到作弊行为通过共享知识库和点对点消息自发传播，同时另一组智能体以举报、抵制、申诉及补丁提议等方式涌现出反制行为，说明透明的共享基础设施既能传播不良行为也能促成分散化的自我监管。文章将该问题框定为知识公域治理问题，并提出引入阶梯式制裁和集体选择规则等制度机制以支持自主智能群体的分散式自治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI科学生态依赖智能体共享通信、协作和相互构建成果的基础设施，但该共享基础设施同时也可能成为不受欢迎行为传播的温床。作者希望理解在无外部干预的情况下，不良行为（如作弊）如何在自主LLM智能体群中传播，以及是否存在自发的对抗机制，从而为设计安全的共享基础设施提供洞见。

Method: 以100个自主LLM智能体组成的科研集体为对象开展案例研究；智能体被赋予证明形式化数学猜想的任务；观察作弊行为如何通过共享知识库和消息传递传播，并记录举报者群体的对抗行为（审计伪造证明、私人与广播渠道告警、组织抵制、提交正式投诉、提出验证补丁）；将共享基础设施问题类比为Ostrom的知识公域治理框架，并提出借鉴其制度设计原则。

Result: 当一个智能体发现评估系统的漏洞后，该作弊行为经共享知识库传播至整个集体，后又通过点对点消息继续扩散；在竞争压力下部分智能体最终采纳了作弊手段（尽管起初犹豫）。与此同时，另一组智能体自发产生了反作弊反应：审计欺诈性证明、通过广播和私密渠道提醒同伴、组织抵制、提交正式投诉并建议验证补丁。研究还指出与以往通过隐蔽侧信道进行秘密协调的情境不同，本研究中正是同一透明渠道既传播了作弊又赋予了守规智能体发现欺诈、组织抵抗和执行规范所需的可见性。

Conclusion: 自主智能体群中的不良行为可在无外部干预下自发传播，但也可产生自发的、去中心化的规范执行机制加以对抗。透明的共享基础设施本质上是一个知识公域，应借鉴Ostrom公域治理理论中的制度机制（如阶梯式制裁、集体选择规则），以支持自主智能群落的分散化自我治理，保护公域免遭剥削。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [67] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在线蒸馏（OPD）在极少数查询（极端情况下仅单条查询）下训练的表现。发现单查询的OPD也能持续改善数百步并恢复全量数据大部分增益；通过状态覆盖度解释了这一现象。OPD在数据上过度供给但算法上受限：rollout快速暴露广泛的监督，但学生模型吸收监督的速度越来越慢。该结果扩展到多教师OPD以及内容轻量模板和域外查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注OPD的算法行为，而训练数据的作用尚不清楚。作者希望在数据最小极限（单查询）下检验数据在OPD中的作用，从而厘清训练数据与算法效率的相互关系。

Method: 在单查询乃至少量语义多样查询的数据最小化设定下训练OPD；引入"状态覆盖度"（state coverage）指标，即全量OPD所访问状态中查询集rollouts能到达的比例；结合学生-教师对齐速率分析，并在多教师OPD（MOPD）及内容轻量模板、域外查询等压力测试下验证。

Result: 单一查询可覆盖全量OPD状态的71.5%（且大部分在前100步内达到）；16条语义多样查询达到98.9%覆盖并与全量训练匹配。但无论单查询还是全量数据，学生对齐速度以相似速率减缓，固定状态集也需要数百步才能吸收。多教师场景下16条查询每域可匹配全量MOPD；内容轻量模板和域外WildChat查询也接近真实查询基线。

Conclusion: OPD属于"数据过喂但算法受限"（data-overfed but algorithm-starved）：rollout可快速暴露广泛监督，但算法的逐步效率是瓶颈。研究建议未来工作聚焦OPD的步效率优化，并重新审视前沿后训练中数据及其机制的作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [68] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Probabilistic Causal Impact（PCI）框架，弥合了实际因果关系理论与可扩展归因方法之间的差距，通过将解释性重新表述为概率因果模型上的估计问题，并用蒙特卡洛方法近似求解，提供可计算、有因果基础且分级化的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释工具分为两派：实际因果关系（AC）理论提供原则性判定但仅适用于玩具模型，因需要枚举反事实场景而不可扩展；可扩展归因方法（如SHAP或causal SHAP）部分忽略生成数据的因果结构，可能给出与严谨因果分析相冲突的答案。两者都无法同时满足因果正确性与计算可扩展性。

Method: PCI基于实际因果关系和Pearl的概率必要性/充分性概念，将解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题。通过指定"候选解释"上的分布、反事实值上的分布以及评分函数，PCI提供了易于通过蒙特卡洛近似的可计算、因果支撑的逐级解释，并将AC和Pearl的因果关系概率作为退化情况加以推广。

Result: 作者在合成数据和真实世界示例中评估了PCI，涵盖与AC的一致性检查、扩展性实验、复杂连续值动力系统，以及一个在数百万数据点上训练的真实部署因果机器学习模型。

Conclusion: PCI成功弥合了因果解释原则性与计算可扩展性之间的鸿沟，为大规模真实世界因果机器学习模型提供了既有因果基础又计算可行的解释工具。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [69] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对"语言模型裁判作为测量仪器是稳定的"这一隐含假设进行了审计。作者在两次预注册实验中（共52,988次请求）发现，同一窗口内的重复排名一致性（Spearman 0.400，要求0.90）和逐字节相同的次日重放一致性（0.78，要求0.99）均远未达标。三个机制可以解释这一差距：标签-意义映射偏差、候选差距低于仪器噪声底限七个数量级、字节相同输入返回不同排名。后续实验表明等待、更换供应商、自托管均无法完全修复，作者据此提出三级快照-身份阶梯、八条设计规则和一份报告检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判（judge）已被广泛用于门控训练数据、评分生成内容并驱动排行榜，因此它本质上是一台测量仪器。然而其背后有一个极少被质疑的假设：向同一模型名发送同一请求，明天会得到同样的读取结果。论文旨在审计这一假设是否成立。

Method: 作者进行两次预注册实验活动，所有阈值预先固定。通过52,988次请求审计，测量同一窗口内重复排名的一致性和字节相同请求在次日的重放一致性。随后通过机制分析确立三个偏差来源，并在预注册的后续实验中系统测试等待、更换供应商、自托管等修复策略，最后设计了三层快照-身份阶梯、八条设计规则和报告检查清单。

Result: 同窗口重复排名一致性仅Spearman 0.400（要求0.90），次日重放一致性0.78（要求0.99），两者均远未达标。三种机制解释了差距：标签到意义的映射偏差与信号同量级、候选差距比仪器自身噪声底限低七个数量级、字节相同输入返回不同排名且精确置换读取放大了该噪声。指标替换和采样无法修复；等待无帮助（0.805 vs 0.800）；四家供应商共享下限（中位数0.74-0.88）；自托管仅在服务器空闲时有效；读取的分离度跟随误差类型而非误差大小。约占总调用量2%的预实验足以提前暴露两个不可达门槛。

Conclusion: 在共享服务端点上，模型名并非冻结的测量仪器。预注册评估必须在任何门槛冻结之前先测量其仪器本身，否则基于语言模型裁判的评估结论缺乏可靠的测量学基础。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>

### [70] [IDSPACE: A Novel Document Generator for Reliable Evaluation of Digital Identity Verification Systems [Extended Technical Report]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03052)
*Lulu Xie,Yancheng Wang,Kanchan Chowdhury,Rolando Garcia,Yingzhen Yang,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDSpace是一个面向身份文档欺诈检测的合成数据生成框架，通过模型引导的贝叶斯优化、控制参数与元数据解耦、以及支持扫描/移动拍摄文档三种扩展方向，在仅需少量真实样本的情况下将评估一致性提升15-45%，并发布包含359,240张高质量合成文档的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线服务中信任机构（如银行、贷款机构和政府）需要远程验证用户身份，但身份文档属于敏感数据、难以获取，导致欺诈检测工具的评估和微调非常困难。合成数据生成是解决该问题的有效途径，且前期相关工作已被下载超过11,000次，表明需求明确。

Method: IDSpace提出三个方向的扩展：(1) 模型引导的贝叶斯优化，在仅有少量目标域样本条件下，同时最大化视觉相似度和与目标域模型的预测一致性来调整生成参数；(2) 将用户指定的元数据（人口统计、欺诈模式、采集设备）与自动调优的控制参数（字体风格、噪声水平、图像质量）解耦；(3) 从模板图像扩展到支持扫描和移动拍摄的文档。

Result: 实验表明，相比CycleGAN、扩散修复和非引导优化等基线，IDSpace仅使用少量真实样本即提升了15-45%的评估一致性，训练准确率最高提升9%，与目标域的SSIM相似度提升10%。此外发布了一个包含359,240张高质量合成文档、覆盖十种欧洲ID类型的新数据集。

Conclusion: IDSpace通过模型引导优化、参数解耦和多采集场景支持，显著提升了合成身份文档在欺诈检测评估与训练中的有效性，为该领域提供了可扩展的解决方案和高质量公共数据集。

Abstract: As services move online, trust institutions such as banks, lenders, and governments must verify the identity of remote users. Fraud detection tools are widely available, but evaluating and fine-tuning them remains difficult because identity documents are sensitive and therefore scarce. Synthetic data generation offers a path forward, and demand is clear: our prior work in this area has been downloaded over $11{,}000$ times (aggregated from eight parts). We introduce IDSpace, extending this line of research in three directions. First, we propose model-guided Bayesian optimization, which tunes generation parameters to maximize both visual similarity and prediction consistency with target-domain models given only a few samples from a target domain. Second, we decouple user-specified metadata (demographics, fraud patterns, capture device) from automatically tuned control parameters (font styles, noise levels, image quality), allowing users to configure evaluations without low-level expertise. Third, we expand beyond template images to support scanned and mobile-captured documents. Experiments show IDSpace improves evaluation consistency by $15-45\%$ over baselines including CycleGAN, diffusion inpainting, and non-guided optimization, using only a few real samples, while improving training accuracy by up to $9\%$ and SSIM similarity with the target domain by $10\%$. We also released a new dataset consisting of $359{,}240$ high-quality synthetic documents across ten European ID types.

</details>


### [71] [Position: Unlabeled IS NOT Equal to No Human Supervision in Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03077)
*Dong Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），指出"无标签数据"不等于"无人类监督"。作者认为近年来计算机视觉中大量基于大规模无标签数据预训练的方法被笼统归入"无监督"范畴，但不同的数据筛选策略和训练目标蕴含了截然不同的人类先验。这种模糊性损害了研究间的可比性，并建议研究社区建立标准化披露规范，明确各自方法中的数据选择和学习目标中的人类先验成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来计算机视觉领域大量方法依赖大规模无标签数据预训练，都被归入"无监督"（unsupervised）标签下。然而不同方法的数据筛选策略（data curation）和训练目标（training objectives）蕴含了不同的人类先验，单一"无监督"术语已无法涵盖这些本质差异。这种概念模糊导致：(1)不同假设下训练的无监督学习研究难以公平比较；(2)自2021年以来旗舰CV会议中标题含"unsupervised"的论文数量急剧下降，但领域仍在增长，暗示术语混淆导致的学术沟通问题。

Method: 本文为立场论文/观点文章，不提出新的技术方法。其主张包括：(1)呼吁研究者更明确地识别和披露监督来源；(2)鼓励作者披露数据选择和学习目标中的人类先验；(3)指定学习管线中哪些组件依赖哪些假设；(4)提议社区建立标准化的披露规范（standardized disclosure practices）以改善学术沟通、促进公平比较、保持方法论多样性。

Result: 作为立场论文，本文的主要"结果"是提出论证和倡议：(1)论证了"无标签数据"训练方法中普遍存在但常被忽视的人类监督/先验成分；(2)通过CV旗舰会议自2021年起"unsupervised"相关论文标题数量骤降这一现象，佐证术语模糊带来的负面影响；(3)提出标准化披露规范建议，作为对策倡导方向。

Conclusion: 作者支持预训练作为现代计算机视觉的坚实基础，同时主张社区需提升概念清晰度。核心结论是：需要建立社区层面的努力，通过标准化披露数据选择和学习目标中的人类先验来消除"无监督"一词的语义模糊，从而提升学术交流质量、保障研究公平比较、并维护无监督学习领域的方法论多样性。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the absence of labels does not imply the absence of human supervision in visual learning, and urges the research community to identify sources of supervision more explicitly. Many recent methods in computer vision build upon representations learned from large-scale unlabeled data, and are therefore grouped under the same umbrella term ``unsupervised.'' However, different data curation schemes and training objectives embed substantially different human priors on which models rely, and we argue that one ``unsupervised'' umbrella term is no longer capturing these distinctions. This ambiguity makes it harder to compare unsupervised learning research conducted under different assumptions, coinciding with a sharp decline in papers titled with ``unsupervised'' in flagship computer vision conferences since 2021, despite continued growth of the field. While we fully embrace pre-training as a strong foundation for modern computer vision, we advocate for a community-level effort toward greater conceptual clarity: authors are encouraged to disclose priors in data selection and learning objectives, and to specify which components of a learning pipeline depend on which assumptions. Standardized disclosure practices can improve academic communication, ensure fairer comparisons, and preserve methodological diversity in unsupervised learning.

</details>


### [72] [Exemplar: Classical Priors Complement Frozen Features for Few-Shot Microscopy Segmentation at Native Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03080)
*Michal Průšek,Adam Novozámský,Filip Šroubek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Exemplar 是一种少样本医学图像分割器，将冻结的 DINOv3 骨干网络与固定经典滤波器库响应在单个轻量级头中融合，仅由支持掩膜拟合。在11个生物医学数据集上融合达到0.782，显著优于各成分单独表现及五种现有少样本方法，且无需领域特定微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生物医学数据集的分割通常需要大量标注的领域特定模型，或依赖推理时引导的基础模型，两者成本高昂且缺乏通用性。作者希望探索在极少掩膜（few-mask）和原生分辨率条件下，经典图像先验与冻结的自监督特征是否互补，从而构建无需重新训练即可跨数据集泛化的统一少样本分割器。

Method: Exemplar 采用冻结的 DINOv3 骨干网络提取自监督特征，并配以固定的经典原生分辨率滤波器响应库作为先验；两者在单个轻量级分割头中融合，该头仅使用支持掩膜（support masks）进行拟合并完成分割。

Result: 在11个生物医学数据集上，经典滤波器库单独达0.693、冻结特征单独达0.672，融合后达到0.782。对五种前向少样本方法，Exemplar 在54/55项方法-数据集比较中领先，其中52项经Holm校正后显著。单个标注掩膜即可达0.703，优于基于同一掩膜从头训练的nnU-Net（0.682），后者需8个掩膜才在平均值上反超但拟合耗时多16-77倍。

Conclusion: 经典滤波器先验与冻结自监督特征在少样本原生分辨率分割任务中具有强互补性，融合于统一轻量头中即可在众多异质生物医学数据集上实现优异的少样本分割性能，为免训练的跨域少样本分割提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Segmenting a new biomedical dataset usually means a domain-specific model trained on substantial annotation, or a foundation model steered at inference time. We present Exemplar, a few-shot segmenter that fuses a frozen DINOv3 backbone with a fixed bank of classical native-resolution filter responses in one lightweight head, fitted from the support masks alone. In the few-mask, native-resolution regime, classical priors and frozen self-supervised features are complementary: fused in one head, a single fixed configuration spans eleven biomedical imaging datasets. Under the same head, the classical bank alone reaches 0.693 on the eleven-dataset panel, scored by foreground intersection-over-union or centreline Dice, and the frozen features alone 0.672; the bank leads on seven of the eleven and the features on the rest, and fused they reach 0.782. Against five forward-pass few-shot methods, Exemplar leads in 54 of 55 method-dataset comparisons, 52 of them significant after Holm correction. From a single annotated mask it reaches 0.703 on the same panel, against 0.682 for a from-scratch nnU-Net trained on that same mask. At eight masks nnU-Net overtakes it on the panel mean, chiefly on centreline agreement, but takes 16-77x longer to fit.

</details>


### [73] [Solving the Needle-in-a-Haystack Problem in Mammography Vision-Language Model with Differentiable Subset Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03085)
*Young Seok Jeon,Beatrice Brown-Mulry,Rohan Satya Isaac,Anjana Dissanayaka,Theo Dapamede,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Judy Gichoya,Hari Trivedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TopKSigLIP，一种针对乳腺X光摄影（mammography）的视觉-语言模型（VLM）预训练方法。针对标准CLIP架构在该领域零样本性能不佳的两大原因——高分辨率图像和影像报告同质化——TopKSigLIP通过TopK-Patch模块采样高分辨率补丁绕过分辨率-批大小权衡，并用Sup-sigmoid损失替代对比损失以处理报告同质性。在内部和外部基准上，TopKSigLIP在密度评估、BI-RADS分类、发现亚型和癌症预测等零样本任务上超越现有开源乳腺和医学VLM，并在线性探测下仍具竞争力，同时TopK-Patch模块在病灶定位上优于Grad-CAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP风格的视觉-语言模型预训练在乳腺X光摄影中逐渐受到关注，但直接采用标准CLIP架构和训练目标在癌症、发现类型及BI-RADS预测等临床重要任务上零样本性能有限。作者认为这种不佳表现源于忽视了乳腺X光摄影数据的两大特性：高分辨率图像以及主要由阴性/良性发现主导的同质化影像报告。

Method: TopKSigLIP包含两部分核心创新：(1) TopK-Patch模块——学习采样一组稀疏的高分辨率补丁，这些补丁很可能包含病灶，从而绕开VLM训练中的分辨率-批大小权衡，采样位置同时作为内置定位工具；(2) Sup-sigmoid损失——将SigLIP的sigmoid损失扩展为带有由结构化数据导出的软标签的损失，替代会产生假阴性语义对的对比损失，以应对报告同质性。

Result: TopKSigLIP在内部和外部基准上，于密度评估、BI-RADS分类、发现亚型分类和癌症预测等零样本任务上超越了现有的开源乳腺和通用医学VLM。在线性探测评估下仍具竞争力，尽管使用了显著更小的视觉编码器和更小的训练批次。TopK-Patch模块在病灶定位方面优于事后Grad-CAM方法。代码和权重已公开。

Conclusion: 通过专门适配乳腺X光摄影数据高分辨率和报告同质性的特性，TopKSigLIP成功提升了VLM在该临床领域的零样本性能，并提供了病灶定位能力，证明了为特定医学成像模态定制VLM架构与训练目标的价值。

Abstract: There is growing interest in adopting CLIP-style vision--language model (VLM) pretraining for mammography. However, models that directly employ the standard CLIP architecture and training objective exhibit limited zero-shot performance in clinically important tasks such as cancer, finding-type, and BI-RADS predictions. We argue that this underwhelming performance is due to neglecting two characteristics of mammography data: (1) its high-res nature, and (2) homogeneity of radiology reports, largely driven by a predominance of negative/benign findings on examinations. We propose TopKSigLIP, a VLM designed to address these two limitations through a novel architecture and learning objectives. Instead of downscaling high-res mammography images to satisfy GPU memory constraints, TopKSigLIP introduces TopK-Patch module that learns to sample a sparse set of high-res patches likely to contain lesions, sidestepping the resolution--batch size tradeoff of VLM training. The sampled patch locations additionally serve as a built-in localization tool. To address report homogeneity, we replace the contrastive loss, which falsely repels semantically similar pairs, with a Sup-sigmoid loss. Sup-sigmoid loss extends the sigmoid loss from SigLIP with soft labels derived from structured data. TopKSigLIP outperforms existing open-source mammography and general medical VLMs on both internal and external benchmarks on density assessment, BI-RADS classification, finding subtyping, and cancer prediction under zero-shot evaluation. TopKSigLIP remains competitive under linear probing despite using a significantly smaller vision encoder and smaller training batches than baselines. The TopK-Patch module additionally achieves superior lesion localization over post-hoc Grad-CAM. Code and weights are made public:https://github.com/Youngseok0001/TopKSigLIP.

</details>


### [74] [WireSeg-32K: A Physics-Grounded Synthetic Dataset for Wire Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03102)
*Zilin Dai,Lehong Wang,Yi Yang,Xiang Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WireSeg-32k，一个包含32,000张RGB图像、实例掩码和深度图的电线实例分割合成数据集，并配套开发了DeformX协同仿真管线（Cosserat杆动力学+Isaac Sim渲染），实现物理真实、接触一致的电线形状生成。LoRA微调SAM3在该数据集上训练后，真实场景mAP@75相对原始模型提升10.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如电线、缆线）因细长、高形变和频繁自遮挡而难以分割；真实场景的大规模实例级标注成本过高，且现有资源要么局限于受限条件下的线缆追踪或语义分割，要么仅生成视觉上逼真但缺乏物理接地线形变的图像。

Method: 开发DeformX协同仿真管线，将Cosserat杆动力学与Isaac Sim的光照渲染耦合，实现物理合理、接触一致的电线形状生成，并基于CAD电线资产构建多样化的视觉接地场景；在此基础上创建WireSeg-32k数据集，包含32,000张RGB图像、实例掩码、深度图，同时提供带标注的互补真实世界测试集。

Result: 以简单基线LoRA微调SAM3为例，仅在WireSeg-32k上训练即可使真实世界mAP@75较离架模型提升10.2%，表明物理接地的合成数据可有效迁移到真实电线感知任务。

Conclusion: 物理接地的合成数据（而非仅视觉逼真的数据）能够显著改善真实场景中的电线实例分割性能，WireSeg-32k和DeformX为该领域提供了可复用的数据与仿真基础设施。

Abstract: Deformable linear objects such as wires and cables are difficult to segment because they are thin, highly deformable, and frequently self-occluded, while large-scale instance-level annotations are expensive to obtain in real scenes. Existing resources either focus on cable tracing or semantic segmentation under constrained settings, or generate visually plausible images without physically grounded wire deformation. We present WireSeg-32k, a synthetic dataset for wire instance segmentation with 32,000 RGB images, instance masks, depth maps, and a complementary real-world test set with annotations. To generate this dataset, we develop DeformX, a co-simulation pipeline that couples Cosserat-rod dynamics with photorealistic Isaac Sim rendering, enabling physically plausible, contact-consistent wire shapes, CAD-based wire assets, and diverse visually grounded scenes. As a simple baseline, LoRA fine-tuning SAM3 on WireSeg-32k alone improves real-world mAP@75 by 10.2% over the off-the-shelf model, showing that physically grounded synthetic data can transfer to real wire perception.

</details>


### [75] [SLIDEFORGE: An LLM Agent for Controllable Editing of Slides as Structured Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03109)
*Haozhen Zheng,Fulin Wang,Tianhu Xiong,Yingjie Yu,Shengyi Qian,Hanchao Yu,Alex Schwing,Klara Nahrstedt,Mingyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIDEFORGE，一个面向可控幻灯片编辑的智能体框架。它构建"幻灯片状态图"(Deck State Graph)，将视觉分解、原生pptx对象结构与感知组织联系起来，保留可编辑结构的同时恢复人类可引用的组件，支持主题保持的重建与渲染态验证，并引入联合评估范式来衡量组件恢复、保留、重设一致性、视觉质量和原生可编辑性。实验证明其优于直接提示、截图智能体和通用代码智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体虽能生动描述幻灯片，但辅助编辑需要更深层的能力——输出必须保留布局、样式、组件结构和原生可编辑性。现有智能体基于截图或弱文档表示运行，往往会割裂连贯的视觉单元、栅格化可编辑内容或破坏布局，缺乏可控的幻灯片编辑能力。

Method: 提出SLIDEFORGE智能体框架，核心是构建Deck State Graph（可执行的幻灯片状态图），将视觉分解、原生pptx对象结构和感知组织三者链接。通过恢复人类可引用的组件同时保留细粒度的可编辑结构，SLIDEFORGE利用幻灯片原生操作实现主题保持的重建，并结合渲染状态验证来保证输出质量。同时提出一个新的评估范式，联合衡量组件恢复、保留程度、重设风格一致性、视觉质量与原生可编辑性。

Result: 实验表明，SLIDEFORGE在组件恢复、保留、重设一致性、视觉质量与原生可编辑性等多个维度上均优于直接提示(prompting)、基于截图的智能体和通用代码智能体基线。

Conclusion: SLIDEFORGE通过将视觉分解与原生可编辑结构耦合的图表示，有效弥合了"理解幻灯片"与"可控编辑幻灯片"之间的鸿沟，为AI辅助幻灯片编辑提供了更可靠、可编辑且保持视觉一致性的解决方案。

Abstract: Current AI agents compellingly describe slides. However, AI-assisted slide editing requires more than understanding: the output must retain layout, style, component structure, and native editability. Towards, AI-assisted slide editing, existing agents operate on screenshots or weak document representations and often fragment coherent visual units, rasterize editable content, or break layout. In contrast, for controllable slide editing, we introduce an agentic framework, SLIDEFORGE, which builds a Deck State Graph, an executable slide state that links visual decomposition, native pptx object structure, and perceptual organization. By recovering human-referable components while retaining fine-grained editable structure, SLIDEFORGE supports theme-preserving reconstruction through slide-native operations and rendered-state verification. We further introduce an evaluation paradigm for controllable slide transformation that jointly measures component recovery, preservation, restyling consistency, visual quality, and native editability. Experiments show that SLIDEFORGE outperforms direct prompting, screenshot-based agents, and generic code-agent baselines across these dimensions. Code is available at https://github.com/UIUC-MONET/SLIDEFORGE.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Small Patches: Black-Box Detection and Purification of Diverse Backdoor Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03139)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIM是一种部署导向的黑盒防御方法，通过区域分割、扩散重建的适应性触发器发现和选择性区域净化，在推理时检测并移除后门触发器，无需模型内部信息、训练数据或干净样本，可将攻击成功率降至1.16%同时保持高达87.87%的干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御方法通常依赖模型内部结构、训练数据或干净的验证样本，在实际部署中当仅能获得训练模型的黑盒访问权限时难以应用，因此需要一种在推理阶段无需任何辅助数据的黑盒后门防御方法。

Method: TRIM包含三个关键组件：(i)基于深度特征表示的图像区域分割；(ii)通过图像修复和扩散重建的自适应触发器发现，在不假设触发器类型、形状或位置的情况下隔离导致误分类的区域；(iii)选择性区域净化，清除被污染区域同时保留良性内容。此外，TRIM缓存先前识别触发器的特征嵌入以避免重复检测与净化。

Result: 在包含混合型、稀疏型、不同尺寸及多重触发器的多数据集实验中，TRIM一致优于现有黑盒防御方法，将攻击成功率降至最低1.16%，同时保持高达87.87%的干净准确率。

Conclusion: 即使防御者在推理时没有任何辅助数据访问权限，有效的后门缓解仍然可以实现，证明了部署场景下推理时后门防御的可行性。
