When $\mathcal X=[0,1]^d$ is equipped with the $\ell^\infty$ norm, $\hat{f}$ admits algorithmic ReLU-MLP and exact ReLU-multi-head transformer realizations of depth $\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$ with $\mathcal{O}(N)$ nonzero parameters.

</details>


### [206] [ALRA: Adaptive Local Relational Alignment for Logit-Based Pre-training Distillation of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03355)
*Quang Hoang Trung,Quang Huu Hieu,Nguyen Van Hoang Phuc,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ALRA是一种面向自回归语言模型的基于logit的知识蒸馏框架，结合学生候选token与教师引导token（锚点），通过自适应局部散度和学生加权成对关系对齐来提升蒸馏效果。在The Pile上用随机初始化的200M和500M参数学生模型进行实验，ALRA分别超越最强蒸馏基线0.94和0.83个百分点，相比无蒸馏预训练分别提升2.31和2.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于logit的知识蒸馏方法通常在整个词表上对齐教师与学生的下一token分布（全局目标），这忽略了似然token候选之间的相对偏好。已有的局部方法仅从教师或学生一方选择候选token：教师单独选择会遗漏学生认为高可能的token，而学生单独选择在训练早期依赖不准确的排序。作者旨在解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出ALRA框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 位置特定的候选集构建：在每个有效预测位置，由学生提议高可能token，同时将教师最可能的token作为锚点纳入候选集；根据教师在当前候选集内的概率分布的广散程度（相对当前批次）自适应调整选取的token数量。(2) 自适应局部散度（Adaptive Local Divergence）：保留质量匹配项（mass-matching term），并分别匹配选定与剩余词表区域的相对token分布；与精确的全词表分解不同，它用单位系数替代两个条件项的教师质量系数，防止因区域的教师概率低而使某项被过度降权。(3) 学生加权成对关系对齐（Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment）：对具有较小学生概率差的高概率token对给予更高权重，对低概率或明显分离的token对给予较低权重。

Result: 在The Pile数据集上，使用随机初始化的200M和500M参数学生模型，在九个零样本基准上平均准确率分别达到36.62%和37.40%。相比最强的竞争蒸馏基线，ALRA分别超过0.94和0.83个百分点；相比无蒸馏的预训练，分别提升2.31和2.91个百分点。

Conclusion: ALRA有效解决了现有局部知识蒸馏中选择候选token的单向局限（仅教师或仅学生），通过结合学生提议与教师锚点，并设计自适应的局部散度与加权成对关系对齐策略，显著提升了蒸馏预训练的自回归语言模型在下游零样本基准上的性能。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation for autoregressive language models usually aligns teacher and student next-token distributions over the entire vocabulary. However, this global objective overlooks relative preferences among likely token alternatives. Existing local approaches often select candidate tokens from either the teacher or the student alone. Teacher-only selection can miss tokens that the student considers likely, while student-only selection can rely on an inaccurate ranking early in training. We propose Adaptive Local Relational Alignment (ALRA), a position-specific framework combining student proposals with teacher guidance. At each valid prediction position, the student proposes likely tokens, while the teacher's most probable token is included as an anchor. ALRA adjusts the number of selected tokens according to how broadly the teacher distributes probability within this candidate set relative to the current batch. Adaptive Local Divergence retains the mass-matching term and separately matches the relative token distributions within the selected and remaining vocabulary regions. Unlike the exact full-vocabulary decomposition, it replaces the teacher-mass coefficients of the two conditional terms with unit coefficients, preventing either term from being downweighted solely because its region has low teacher probability. Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment emphasizes high-probability token pairs with small student probability gaps and gives less weight to unlikely or clearly separated pairs. Experiments on The Pile with randomly initialized 200M- and 500M-parameter students across nine zero-shot benchmarks yield average accuracies of 36.62% and 37.40%. ALRA exceeds the strongest competing distillation baseline by 0.94 and 0.83 percentage points and improves over pre-training without distillation by 2.31 and 2.91 points, respectively.

</details>


### [207] [Towards a Statistical Understanding of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03501)
*Siyuan He,Bokai Yang,Jie Hu,Ziwen Gao,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将混合专家（MoE）架构视为一种局部化聚合方法，推导了密集与稀疏路由的Oracle风险界，分离了近似、专家学习和路由估计误差，并从输入空间几何角度解释了门控机制与共享专家（如DeepSeekMoE）的作用，建立了一个统一理解MoE的统计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在现代大规模模型中日益重要，但其设计选择（路由、稀疏激活、共享专家）的统计作用仍缺乏深入理解。现有理论多聚焦于参数化或设定正确的MoE模型，未能刻画泛化能力与计算效率之间的根本权衡，因此需要一个新的理论视角来弥合这一空白。

Method: 作者将MoE重新概念化为一种局部化（localized）聚合形式，推导了针对具有演化专家的密集与稀疏路由的学习Oracle风险界，并将误差分解为近似误差、专家学习误差和路由估计误差三个可分离部分。通过输入空间几何分析门控行为，将路由性能与局部专家优势区域相关联，同时分析稀疏Top-K路由如何在保留局部聚合优势的同时控制每输入计算量，并阐释共享专家如何提取共性预测结构。

Result: 论文刻画了局部化如何重塑近似-估计-计算三方权衡；证明了稀疏Top-K路由能在控制每输入计算的同时保留局部聚合的益处；揭示了路由性能与输入空间中专家局部优势区域之间的几何关系；表明共享专家可提取常见预测结构，使路由专家专注于残差局部变化。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过输入依赖的专家聚合理解MoE提供了一个统一的统计框架，其中专家特化与计算权衡受局部预测结构支配，为MoE的理论分析和架构设计提供了系统化的指导原则。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity by combining a collection of expert predictors through input-dependent routing, while often activating only a small subset of experts for each input. Despite their growing importance in modern large-scale models, the statistical roles of their design choices, especially routing, sparse activation, and shared experts, remain only partially understood, as existing theory has largely focused on parametric or correctly specified MoE models. In this paper, we view MoE as a form of localized aggregation and show how this localization reshapes the approximation-estimation-computation tradeoff. We derive oracle risk bounds for learning dense and sparse routing with evolving experts, separating approximation, expert-learning, and router-estimation errors, and characterize how sparse Top-K routing can retain the benefits of localized aggregation while controlling per-input computation. We also interpret gating through the geometry of input space, relating routing performance to regions of local expert advantage, and show how shared experts, as adopted in architectures such as DeepSeekMoE, can extract common predictive structure so that routed experts focus on residual local variation. Together, these results provide a unified statistical framework for understanding MoE through input-dependent expert aggregation, in which expert specialization and computational tradeoffs are governed by local predictive structure.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Discrete Gromov-Wasserstein Duality: Algorithms and Isomorphism Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03094)
*Gabriel Rioux,Joanna Marks,Riccardo Passeggeri,Ziv Goldfeld*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了适用于所有有限支撑度量测度空间（mm spaces）的Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）距离及其熵正则化形式的对偶结果，基于此进一步推导了经验GW距离的样本复杂度与极限分布，提出了具有形式收敛保证的求解正则化GW问题的新算法，并构建了检验固定节点数图分布同构性的高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW距离通过度量测度空间的内在结构实现对齐，在图比较等场景中具有重要价值。然而，已有的GW对偶形式仅限于带平方欧氏距离或内积成本的欧氏分布，且缺乏对有限支撑mm空间的统一对偶理论，限制了统计推断与算法设计的进一步发展。

Method: 推导适用于所有有限支撑mm空间的GW距离及熵正则化GW距离的新对偶结果；利用该对偶结果推导经验GW距离在有限mm空间之间的样本复杂度及适当中心化和缩放后的极限分布；基于该理论提出求解正则化GW问题的新算法并给出形式收敛保证。

Result: 获得了适用于一般有限支撑mm空间的GW对偶理论，建立了经验GW距离的样本复杂度与极限分布，提出了具有严格收敛保证的正则化GW求解算法，并由此构建了基于样本检验固定节点数图上分布同构性的原理性高效框架。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了GW距离对偶理论的适用范围至所有有限支撑mm空间，并为GW距离的统计推断与计算提供了坚实的理论基础与高效算法，最终实现了对图分布同构性假设检验的可行方案。

Abstract: The Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distance provides a principled framework for aligning metric measure (mm) spaces based solely on their intrinsic structure. Its ability to identify isomorphic representations of distributions across spaces renders it valuable for comparing data where equality up to isomorphism occurs naturally such as in graphs or, more generally, distributions on graphs. Recently, a type of dual form for the GW distance between Euclidean distributions with the squared Euclidean or inner product costs was derived, spurring the development of new statistical and algorithmic results for this setting. This work furnishes a novel duality result for GW distances with and without entropic regularization that is applicable to all finitely supported mm spaces. Leveraging this result, we derive the sample complexity of empirical GW distances between finite mm spaces, as well as limit distributions under proper centering and scaling. Furthermore, we propose new algorithms for solving the regularized GW problem which are subject to formal convergence guarantees. These statistical and algorithmic advancements give rise to a principled and efficient framework for testing whether two distributions on the set of graphs with a fixed number of nodes are isomorphic based on samples.

</details>


### [209] [Equivariance, Curvature and Symmetry in Functional Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03042)
*Jocelyn Nembe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文刻画了稀疏函数型数据局部线性协方差估计在重参数化（重新配准/时间尺度变换）下的等变性。总体层面协方差算子在任意微分同胚重参数化下酉共轭；但估计层面只有仿射变换才保证精确交换性。对于一般C^{2,1}微分同胚，偏离等变性的误差由归一化曲率κ_ψ控制，因此零曲率恰是统计等变性的边界。此外，有限群对称性作为正交投影正则化器，其风险增益等于反不变估计误差减去对称误设定平方；轨道协方差恒等式量化了方差缩减。这些原理可传导至特征值、特征空间及截断PACE预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型数据的统计分析（如均值、协方差估计）在实践前常常经过时间尺度变换、注册或重参数化，但以往不清楚所得推断在何种条件下不依赖于所选坐标系（即具有坐标等变性）。作者希望从理论上严格刻画局部线性协方差估计在重参数化下的等变性边界，揭示总体层面与估计层面之间坐标不变性的本质差异，并进一步理解群对称性对估计风险的规整化作用。

Method: 作者在总体与估计两个层面分别建立协方差估计的重参数化交换性刻画。①总体层面利用协方差算子在微分同胚重参数化下的酉共轭性建立不变框架；②估计层面推导局部线性协方差估计器在重参数化下的交换条件的精确表征，证明仅当重参数化为仿射时普遍等变性成立；③对一般光滑微分同胚，推导与归一化曲率κ_ψ相关的局部线性缺陷的渐近界（含带宽项与局部样本量项）；④引入有限群对称性，将对称影响刻画为正交投影正则化，建立轨道协方差恒等式并量化离得方差缩减与群大小的关系。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 总体协方差算子在任意微分同胚重参数化下具有酉共轭不变性；(2) 在估计层面，精确的交换/等变性仅当重参数化是仿射时普遍成立；(3) 对一般C^{2,1}微分同胚ψ，局部线性估计缺陷为O_P{κ_ψ(h²+h n_loc^{-1/2}+h₀²+h₀ n_{loc,1}^{-1/2})}，其中κ_ψ=‖ψ''/ψ'‖_∞为归一化曲率，故曲率非零导致统计等变性破坏；(4) 有限群对称性作为正交投影正则化器，风险增益=反不变估计误差−对称误设定平方；(5) 轨道协方差恒等式表明方差缩减不由群大小单独决定。以上结论推广至特征值、特征空间及截断PACE预测。

Conclusion: 本文严格区分了总体层面的坐标不变性与统计平滑引入的几何障碍：重参数化在总体层面对协方差算子保持酉共轭，但在局部线性估计层面只有仿射变换才能实现精确的等变性，零曲率构成统计等变的精确边界。有限群对称性通过正交投影正则化带来可精确量化的风险收益。这些理论结果对含注册/时间尺度的函数型数据推断（特征分解、PACE预测）具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Statistical procedures for functional data are routinely applied after changes of time scale, registration, or other reparametrisations, although it is generally unclear when the resulting inference is independent of the chosen coordinates. We characterize this equivariance for local-linear covariance estimation from sparsely observed functional data. At the population level, covariance operators are unitarily conjugate under every diffeomorphic reparametrisation. At the estimation level, exact commutation holds universally if and only if the reparametrisation is affine. For a general \(C^{2,1}\) diffeomorphism, departure from equivariance is controlled by the normalized curvature \(κ_ψ=\|ψ''/ψ'\|_\infty\), with local-linear defect \[ O_P\!\left\{κ_ψ\left(h^2+h n_{\mathrm{loc}}^{-1/2} +h_0^2+h_0 n_{\mathrm{loc},1}^{-1/2}\right)\right\}. \] Thus zero curvature is exactly the boundary of statistical equivariance. We then show that finite-group symmetry acts as an orthogonal-projection regularizer: its risk gain is exactly the anti-invariant estimation error minus the squared symmetry misspecification. An orbit-covariance identity quantifies the attainable variance reduction and shows why group size alone does not determine the gain. These principles propagate to eigenvalues, eigenspaces and truncated PACE prediction. The results separate coordinate invariance at the population level from the geometric obstructions introduced by statistical smoothing.

</details>


### [210] [G-Exponential Families through Probability Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03208)
*Manuela-Simona Cojocea*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于概率坐标图（probability coordinate charts）的概率几何构造方法，用于构建广义指数族（G-exponential families）。通过将单位区间上的经典指数族经图表传递到值空间，改变了指数结构所表示的几何，但指数函数本身保持不变，这区别于Tsallis和Kaniadakis框架中的代数形变方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的指数族框架在处理重尾分布、鲁棒估计等问题时存在不足，而Tsallis和Kaniadakis等框架通过代数形变指数函数来实现广义化，改变了指数函数本身。作者希望探索一种不同的路径：保持指数函数不变，仅通过改变其所在的几何（坐标图）来生成新的指数族结构，从而获得经典框架所不具备的重尾、鲁棒等性质。

Method: 方法核心是一种几何构造：将单位区间上的经典指数族通过概率坐标图（chart）传递（transport）到值空间。具体而言：(1) 构建G-指数族作为经典指数族沿坐标图的push-forward；(2) 概率坐标经由图与原指数族精确对应；(3) 经典矩与Fisher信息经pullback映射到值空间后对应Kolmogorov矩与Fisher信息；(4) 通过Kolmogorov方差描述Fisher信息；利用相对熵最小化在原概率测度下受坐标矩约束来刻画G-指数族，并证明该transport保持信息几何结构。

Result: (1) 尾部行为由坐标图决定，每个族成员与图的密度尾部等价，重尾源于几何构造且在概率坐标倾斜下阶数不变（与经典指数倾斜相反）；(2) 充分统计量有界，MLE退化为概率坐标下的矩匹配，影响函数有界，鲁棒性源于几何紧致化；(3) 概率重心是均值参数的pullback；(4) Cauchy图示例及Monte Carlo研究验证了即使所有族成员均值无穷大，估计仍然高效、校准且有界影响。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过几何紧致化（改变表示指数结构的几何而非改变指数函数）就能系统性地构建广义指数族，同时获得重尾建模能力与参数估计的鲁棒性，为基础统计推断提供了一种全新的"几何紧致化"范式。

Abstract: We develop a probability geometric construction of generalized exponential families based on probability coordinate charts. A classical exponential family on the unit interval is transported to value space through a chart, producing what we call a G-exponential family. The construction changes the geometry in which exponential structure is represented while leaving the exponential function itself unchanged. This distinguishes it from the algebraic deformations used in the Tsallis and Kaniadakis frameworks.
  The transported families inherit the structure of the probability coordinate framework. The probability coordinate of each family member follows exactly the original exponential family on the unit interval. Initial Kolmogorov moments are therefore obtained by pulling classical coordinate moments back to value space. For the canonical family, the probability barycenter is the pullback of the mean parameter, while the Fisher information coincides with the Kolmogorov variance.
  Tail behaviour is inherited from the chart. Every family member is tail equivalent to the chart density, so heavy tails arise from geometry and remain unchanged in order under probability coordinate tilting. This is the opposite of classical exponential tilting.
  The sufficient statistic is bounded by construction, maximum likelihood reduces to moment matching in probability coordinates, and the maximum likelihood estimator has a bounded influence function. Thus robustness arises from geometric compactification. We also characterise G-exponential families as minimisers of relative entropy to the chart law under coordinate moment constraints and prove that transport preserves the information geometry of the family. A Cauchy chart example and a Monte Carlo study confirm efficient, calibrated, and bounded influence estimation even though every family member has an infinite mean.

</details>


### [211] [Geometric Ergodicity of Affine Invariant Ensemble Langevin and its Discrete Time Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03326)
*Hong Ye Tan,Yifan Chen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射不变集成Langevin动力学的几何遍历性问题。针对经验协方差可能趋于奇异这一核心困难，作者提出了结合逆协方差势垒与强制性指数能量的新颖Lyapunov函数，在势函数满足有界Hessian且球外强凸条件下证明了几何遍历性。同时发现直接使用Euler–Maruyama离散化即使对一维高斯目标也可能以正概率发散，由此引入协方差迹时间正则化，证明了正则化扩散及其离散化（步长足够小）的几何遍历性，并给出了离散化不变分布随步长趋于零时弱收敛到目标乘积分布的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射不变集成采样器在贝叶斯应用中广泛使用，但其定量收敛理论（特别是几何遍历性）一直是悬而未决的基本问题。现有理论仅能证明全变差意义上的定性收敛，核心困难在于经验协方差可能接近奇异，因此需要发展新的分析工具来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Lyapunov函数，将逆协方差势垒与强制的指数能量相结合，用于证明几何遍历性。同时提出一种协方差迹时间正则化方案，并分析其Euler–Maruyama离散化。

Result: 在势函数具有有界Hessian且球外强凸的条件下证明了几何遍历性；揭示了直接Euler–Maruyama离散化即使在一维高斯目标下也可能以正概率发散；证明了正则化扩散及其适当小步长离散化的几何遍历性；建立了离散化不变分布随步长趋于零时弱收敛到目标乘积分布。

Conclusion: 通过上述Lyapunov函数方法与时间正则化策略，填补了仿射不变集成Langevin动力学在几何遍历性理论方面的空白，为该类方法提供了严格的定量收敛保证。

Abstract: Affine-invariant ensemble samplers are widely used in Bayesian applications. However, their quantitative convergence theory, in particular geometric ergodicity, remains a basic open question. We study the affine invariant ensemble Langevin dynamics, an interacting particle system that uses the empirical covariance of the whole ensemble as a preconditioner. While effective in practice, theoretical understanding of this method is not available beyond plain qualitative convergence in total variation; a central difficulty is that the empirical covariance can approach singularity. This paper addresses this challenge. For potentials with bounded Hessian that are strongly convex outside a ball, we prove geometric ergodicity using a novel Lyapunov function that combines an inverse-covariance barrier with a coercive exponential energy. We then show that directly applying the Euler--Maruyama scheme can diverge with positive probability, even for a one-dimensional Gaussian target. This motivates a covariance-trace time regularization. We prove geometric ergodicity of the regularized diffusion and, for sufficiently small step size, of its unadjusted Euler--Maruyama discretization. We also show that the invariant distributions of the discretization converge weakly to the product target distribution as the step size tends to zero.

</details>


### [212] [Symmetries and Causality: Causal Effect Identification Beyond IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03697)
*Martin Rabel,Jakob Runge*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据中保持不变因果机制的对称性来描述统计系统的形式化语言，用于因果推理。该框架在IID数据上能复现标准理论结果，但主要目标是超越IID数据并扩展因果推理的范围，处理do-干预或软干预无法捕获的复杂因果查询，并统一描述c-components、hedges、缺失数据、迁移和鲁棒性等结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自然科学中，对称性和因果效应无处不在，但在复杂的机器学习任务（如强化学习中的世界建模）中难以利用。现有的因果推理框架在IID数据假设下工作良好，但面对超越IID数据以及更复杂的因果查询时存在局限，需要一个更抽象、更通用且能统一已有结果的数学语言。

Method: 提出一种基于对称性的形式化描述，其中对称性体现在数据中保持因果机制不变的变换上。论文为该语言提供了模型和查询的形式化描述，并给出在此形式化体系内从数据中进行数学上严格辨识的正式基础设施和策略。

Result: 该框架在IID数据上能够复现并匹配标准理论结果（包括实验数据和非实验数据的迁移），同时极大地扩展了因果推理的适用范围。它能超越IID数据，处理do-干预或软干预无法表达的复杂因果查询，并且能统一描述c-components、hedges等经典结构，还能涵盖缺失数据，并天然适用于迁移与鲁棒性的描述。

Conclusion: 这种基于对称性保留因果机制的新视角为因果推理提供了抽象而通用的数学语言，在复现已有结果的基础上，显著扩展了因果推理的范围，并为数据建模中因果相关的方面（如迁移、鲁棒性、缺失数据）提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: In the natural sciences, symmetries and cause-effect relationships are ubiquitous. Yet for complex machine-learning tasks, like world-modeling in reinforcement learning, they appear difficult to harness. We propose a formal description of statistical systems based on symmetries in data leaving causal mechanisms invariant. The result is an abstract, simple and general mathematical language for causal reasoning. This paper provides formal descriptions of models and queries, setting up this language, and the formal infrastructure and strategies for their mathematically rigorous identification from data within this formalism. This approach reproduces and matches standard theoretical results on IID data and transport of experimental and non-experimental data. But its main purpose is to unify and substantially extend the scope of causal reasoning, in going beyond IID data and in approaching complex causal queries not captured by do- or soft-interventions. This new perspective on causally relevant aspects of data-modeling additionally sheds new light on well-known structures like c-components or hedges but also includes aspects of missing data and is inherently well-suited for the description of transfer and robustness properties.

</details>


### [213] [One Probability, Two Roles: The Separation of Coherence and Frequency in Adaptive Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04115)
*Yonggang Lu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了"角色分离原则"(Role Separation Principle),系统区分了概率在现代数据分析中的两种不同角色:作为顺序预测和决策的内部一致语言(基于评估律Q),以及作为经验主张(如稳定化、校准、重复抽样有效性)的基础(基于主宰律P和声明的重复制度)。文中综合了前序列预测、校准、鞅与博弈论有效性、自适应数据分析、共形预测和长上下文AI可靠性等领域的成果,提供了两个分离结果,并开发了一个统一的稳定化框架和审计清单,用于澄清校准声明、合成数据有效性及AI输出中的结构不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计设定中,概率的两种角色(内部一致的预测语言 vs 经验有效性的基础)通常由条件正确性、i.i.d.或可交换性假设对齐。但在自适应和AI中介环境中,预测在反馈回路中运行,而可靠性却在可能不同的定律和制度下评估,两种角色可能分化。急需一个形式化的框架来厘清这种分离及其后果,特别是对AI高置信输出可靠性问题的指导。

Method: 作者综合了来自prequential预测、校准理论、鞅与博弈论有效性、自适应数据分析、共形预测及长上下文AI可靠性等多个领域的已有结果,将其融入一个形式化的"角色分离"框架。论文建立了Role Separation Principle,区分了内部一致性、评价律下的条件均值正确性以及目标特定的参考和稳定条件。文中给出两个分离定理:(1)极限频率分布的适当粗化不必然识别主宰律;(2)在该律下条件正确的预测不必然伴随稳定化。最后构建了一个统一的稳定化框架(覆盖基于滤波、迁移、策略-环境和设计四种制度),并通过制度条件化审计清单进行操作化。

Result: 论文提出了Role Separation Principle作为统一的形式化原则,通过两个分离定理证明概率的内部预测角色与外部经验有效性角色在自适应和AI环境中确实可以解耦。作者开发了一个跨越四种制度的统一稳定化框架(滤波基、迁移基、策略-环境、设计基),并通过制度条件化审计清单在实际场景中操作化。应用示例阐明了校准声明如何失效、合成数据的有效性边界,以及高置信但无支持的AI输出背后的结构性不匹配。

Conclusion: 概率在数据驱动实践中的两种角色在经典设定中常被隐式对齐,但在自适应和AI中介场景中必须明确分离处理。Role Separation Principle提供了诊断和规避"角色混淆"的理论工具,而统一的稳定化框架与审计清单为实证声明(如校准、有效性)提供了制度敏感的操作化路径。这些结果对AI系统的可靠性评估和"高置信输出"的合理性审查具有直接的方法论意义。

Abstract: Probability plays two distinct roles in modern data-analytic practice: (i) as an internally coherent, filtration-relative language for sequential forecasting and, together with a stated loss or utility, decision making; and (ii) as a foundation for empirical claims such as stabilization, calibration, and repeated-sampling validity. The first role is relative to an assessment law $\mathbb Q$, whereas the second is evaluated under a governing law $\mathbb P$ and a declared repetition regime. In classical settings the roles are often aligned by conditional correctness under $\mathbb P$ together with i.i.d.\ or other stable-law assumptions under a fixed design, or by exchangeability. In adaptive and AI-mediated settings they can diverge because forecasts operate within feedback loops while reliability is evaluated under a potentially different law and regime. We synthesize relevant results from prequential forecasting, calibration, martingale and game-theoretic validity, adaptive data analysis, conformal prediction, and long-context AI reliability into a formal account of \emph{role separation}. The resulting \emph{Role Separation Principle} distinguishes internal coherence from conditional-mean correctness under the evaluation law and from target-specific reference and stability conditions. Two separation results show that a proper coarsening of the limiting-frequency distribution need not identify the governing law, and that even conditionally correct forecasts under that law need not accompany stabilization. We then develop a unified stabilization framework spanning filtration-based, shift-based, policy--environment, and design-based regimes and operationalize it through a regime-conditional audit checklist. Applied illustrations clarify calibration claims, synthetic-data validity, and structural mismatches relevant to some high-confidence unsupported AI outputs.

</details>


### [214] [Bernstein--von Mises theorems for Bayesian probabilistic numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04124)
*Sascha Gaudlitz,Sven Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯非参数的角度研究求解非线性偏微分方程（PDE）的概率数值方法。在随机配置点上给出来带噪声的评估后，作者在未知解上放置截断的高斯级数先验，并建立了达到极小极大非参数收敛速率（至多差一个对数因子）的收缩性。主要结果给出了正阶Sobolev空间以及（在适当条件下）一致拓扑下的后验高斯近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将概率数值方法与贝叶斯非参数理论相结合，用于求解非线性PDE。与经典不适定反问题不同（其Bernstein-von Mises定理通常需要弱得多的拓扑），这里的观测算子具有微分性质而非光滑化作用，其线性化的反演会增益正则性，从而使得强拓扑下的结果成为可能。

Method: 方法包括：在随机配置点上采用带噪声的观测；在未知解上施加截断的高斯级数先验；建立后验收缩率并证明在正阶Sobolev空间和一致拓扑下后验的高斯近似（Bernstein-von Mises型结果）；后验可以后验均值或后验众数为中心；进一步证明高斯Laplace近似与真实后验在根号N尺度下渐近等价。

Result: 主要结果为：后验收缩达到极小极大非参数速率（至多差对数因子）；在正阶Sobolev空间以及（在合适条件下）一致拓扑下成立Bernstein-von Mises型后验高斯近似；高斯Laplace近似与真实后验以根号N尺度渐近等价。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在求解非线性PDE的概率数值方法框架中，与经典不适定反问题不同，观测算子为微分型而非光滑型，线性化反演能增益正则性，因此能够建立强拓扑（正阶Sobolev空间和一致拓扑）下的后验高斯近似结果。

Abstract: We study probabilistic numerical methods for solving nonlinear PDEs from a Bayesian nonparametric perspective. Given noisy evaluations at random collocation points, we place a truncated Gaussian series prior on the unknown solution and establish contraction at the minimax nonparametric rate, up to a logarithmic factor. Our main results give Gaussian approximations of the posterior in positive-order Sobolev spaces and, under suitable conditions, in the uniform topology. This contrasts with classical ill-posed inverse problems, where Bernstein--von Mises theorems typically require substantially weaker topologies. Here, the observation operator is differential rather than smoothing, and inversion of its linearisation gains regularity, making these strong-topology results possible. The posterior may be centred at either the posterior mean or the posterior mode. We further prove that the Gaussian Laplace approximation is asymptotically equivalent to the true posterior at a $\sqrt{N}$-scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个编码训练语料unigram分布的方向（"ignorance方向"），该方向作为模型不确定时的贝叶斯先验。将最终预测状态投影到此方向可获得token级先验载荷因子λ，该λ随上下文信息量增加而下降，且将预测状态分解为两个正交向量恰好对应tempered Bayesian update中的unigram先验与上下文似然。该方向具有因果活性，可通过调节λ控制模型预测向先验的KL距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时如何做出预测，揭示模型不确定时的内在回退机制（Bayesian先验），并说明该先验的几何来源和可操作性。

Method: 在Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia四个模型家族（0.4B-405B参数）中，通过分析unembedding矩阵的几何结构提取unigram先验方向；将最终预测状态投影至该方向定义每个token的先验载荷因子λ；形式上证明该投影将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，分别对应tempered Bayesian update中的先验（以λ为指数）和似然；通过干预实验验证该方向的因果作用。

Result: 所有四个模型家族均出现"ignorance方向"；λ随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降；该几何分解与tempered Bayesian update精确对应，使λ在不同模型规模和家族间可比较；更大的模型在高上下文极限下先验依赖更低；调节λ可因果地改变预测朝/离unigram先验的KL距离。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测可解释为一种tempered Bayesian更新，其先验（unigram分布）几何编码在unembedding矩阵中，λ作为可校准和可干预的标量提供了一个统一框架来理解、比较和操控不同规模语言模型的预测行为。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [216] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，一种直接优化奖励上尾覆盖的强化学习方法。与传统只优化期望奖励不同，TailRL最大化策略超过随机奖励阈值的对数概率，使梯度更关注稀有高奖励采样，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。仅需对advantage函数做简单修改，即可兼容现有RL流程，在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化任务中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但对于生成式策略，平均值会掩盖一个关键差异：两个策略可以达到相同平均奖励，却产生稀有高奖励rollout的概率截然不同。随着训练和推理时的采样规模增加，保留高奖励结果的概率质量变得尤为重要，因此作者提出直接优化这种覆盖（coverage）而非仅关注期望奖励。

Method: TailRL将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件：对每个奖励阈值，衡量策略超过该阈值的概率。它最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率。其梯度对稀有高奖励rollout给予更大权重，可数学解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。实现上仅需对advantage函数做简单修改，因而可无缝兼容现有强化学习流水线。

Result: 在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等四项任务上，TailRL充分利用了稀有高奖励的训练样本，避免了陷入次优解；同时所得模型在推理阶段随着采样量增加能获得更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: TailRL证明了直接优化奖励上尾覆盖的有效性，为生成式策略在稀有高奖励场景下的强化学习提供了新思路，且实现简洁、与现有RL框架兼容，能够更好地利用推理时增加的采样来获得收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [217] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种将交互历史直接存储在图网络接触边上的颗粒动力学学习模拟器。每条边通过注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久化记忆，并利用边身份字典在接触图变化时保留记忆；物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力、执行库仑摩擦极限并施加等大反向内力。在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE相比GNS和NMGNS将长时程位置误差降低31-62%、最终沉积误差降低58-89%，参数更少、粒子几乎无相互穿透，相比MPM参考求解器分别提速12.2倍和8.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒动力学严重依赖颗粒间接触历史，而现有学习模拟器通常将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，难以在接触不断形成、断裂和重排的过程中保持历史信息，导致长期行为不稳定、不准确。

Method: TRACE在图网络的接触边上直接存储交互历史，每条边维护一个由注意力消息传递和门控循环单元更新的持久化记忆；通过边身份字典在接触图拓扑变化时保留此记忆。解码器采用物理结构设计，分别预测法向与切向接触力，强制执行库仑摩擦极限并施加等大反向内力。训练策略为先单步预训练再自回归展开微调。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE生成稳定且物理一致的长期展开，准确再现最终沉积几何和坍塌动能释放。相比GNS和NMGNS，长时程位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时使用更少参数并维持近零粒子相互穿透；相比MPM参考求解器在2D和3D分别提速12.2倍和8.9倍。

Conclusion: 将交互历史存储在接触边而非节点上的边记忆机制能更有效地捕捉颗粒接触动力学的时间依赖性，显著提升长期rollout的精度与稳定性，同时保持计算高效性，为颗粒材料学习模拟提供了一种新的建模范式。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [218] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），通过从有限输入映射库中自适应选择扭曲函数来修复GP-BO中核函数与真实几何不匹配的问题，在保持高概率收敛保证的同时显著提升样本效率，并在多种基准和20维多智能体系统设计任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB等GP-BO方法具有收敛率保证，但这些保证要求固定核函数。然而在实际问题中，原始坐标常与目标函数的真实几何不匹配（如对数尺度超参数或局部峰值），此时输入扭曲能大幅提升样本效率，但现有GP-UCB证明不允许核动态变化，导致缺乏具有理论保证的自适应输入几何调整方法。

Method: FLIWBO从有限库中选择平滑输入映射（扭曲），其选择规则可依赖任意历史信息。该方法在温和假设下保留高概率收敛保证，并显式给出库大小导致的√(N_ε)代价；算法在探索过程中根据观测动态调整输入几何以加速学习。

Result: 受控诊断实验表明有限库扭曲能修复人为植入的几何失配并识别FLIWBO的失败情形。在四个重复基准（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参优化）中，FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能逃脱甚至Oracle-warp EI都无法解决的陷阱，恢复了手动对数缩放的大部分收益；在允许匹配遗憾保证的方法中表现领先。20维多智能体系统设计实验进一步验证了在昂贵带噪评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO弥合了自适应输入扭曲与理论收敛保证之间的鸿沟，证明在有限库范围内动态调整输入几何既可行又高效，为核几何失配场景下的GP-BO提供了兼具理论保障与实际性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [219] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）空间矢量数据表示为图结构，通过对新旧ENC数据集分别编码为两张图并将变更分类任务建模为图对分类问题，利用多种图神经网络（GNN）架构判断ENC更新对航行安全构成的是关键还是非关键风险，实验表明图表示方法有效提升ENC更新分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENC是用于海洋导航的地理空间矢量数据集，其维护具有挑战性，尤其是根据对航行安全的重要性对ENC中物体的变更进行分类非常关键。然而在空间数据上应用GNN时，哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合该任务尚不明确。

Method: 将ENC空间对象作为图的节点，空间和语义关系作为边来构建图结构；将新旧ENC数据集分别编码为一对图，并把变更分类任务转化为图对分类问题；在此基础上训练并评估多种GNN架构和模型配置，以分类编码后的图对是否构成关键或非关键的航行安全风险。

Result: 基于图的表示方法有效提升了ENC更新分类的准确度，提供了一种可扩展的方案来自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程。

Conclusion: 图神经网络结合图对分类框架能够有效识别ENC更新对航行安全的关键性风险，为ENC维护流程的自动化提供了可行的规模化途径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [220] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对因果基础模型（CFMs）这一新兴领域进行了实践性介绍。CFMs借鉴基础模型范式，通过预训练神经网络在全新的数据集上利用上下文学习直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需针对每个新问题重新设计因果机制或进行模型微调。文章回顾了因果推断与机器学习的必要背景，并辅以示例代码与Jupyter notebooks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断针对每个新问题都需要定制化流程：先提出因果机制、选择合适的估计器、再训练模型，成本高昂且难以规模化。与此同时，机器学习已广泛转向基础模型范式——一次大规模预训练，即可在多种新任务上无需微调地应用。作者希望将这一范式引入因果推断，推动因果基础模型这一新兴方向的发展，并为读者提供实践入口。

Method: 本文并非提出新的模型算法，而是一篇综述/教程性质的工作。其方法在于：系统梳理因果推断与机器学习中支持因果基础模型所需的背景知识，阐述CFM如何利用预训练与上下文感知（in-context）能力在全新数据集上直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应），并通过配套的示例代码和Jupyter notebooks提供可复现的实操指导。

Result: 文章给出了因果基础模型这一新兴领域的实用介绍，构架了从传统定制化因果推断到CFM范式迁移的完整知识体系，区分了CFM与传统方法在「是否需要模型更新/微调」上的核心差异，并通过代码示例辅助读者上手应用CFM进行因果估计。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型代表了将基础模型范式成功迁移到因果推断领域的方向。通过预训练与上下文学习，CFM能够在全新数据集上无需微调地估计因果量，有望摆脱传统方法中"每个问题一条定制流水线"的局限，提升因果推断的通用性与易用性，属于具有前景的新兴研究领域。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [221] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的关系，提出可将模型参数视为对应样本的稠密向量表示。通过基于模型参数矩阵谱特征的向量表示方法，理论分析表明参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值相关联，实验在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST上验证了该方法能高精度区分不同数据子集上训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自编码器参数是否可以作为训练样本的向量表征，从而在不使用原始样本的情况下实现模型间区分与数据表征，弥补传统方法需要复杂向量生成算法或原始数据样本的局限。

Method: 构建自编码器模型（编码器-解码器架构），基于模型参数矩阵的谱特征形成向量表示；通过理论分析推导参数矩阵奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵特征值之间的关系；在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上进行区分实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了自编码器参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值之间存在关联，确保数据空间与参数空间之间的信息传递；实验表明该向量表示方法在不同数据子集训练的模型之间具有高区分精度。

Conclusion: 训练后的自编码器参数可以被视为训练样本的表示形式，无需复杂向量生成算法或原始样本即可实现模型间的有效区分，为模型参数作为数据表征的研究提供了理论与实验支撑。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [222] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力机制在训练中的动态特性，提出注意力索引模型框架，揭示了注意力参数化本身作为架构隐式偏置的作用：直接优化注意力矩阵可能陷入不良状态，而绑定注意力与解绑注意力在对称性破缺和弱恢复机制上表现出截然不同的动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动态尤其在注意力矩阵具有高秩时仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出注意力索引模型框架，可表示多层和多头注意力架构。在高维极限下用有限迹序参数刻画人口损失景观；在线SGD则受无限矩阵矩层级控制，并用有限截断系统进行指数级近似。通过分析直接优化S、绑定注意力S=WW^T和未绑定注意力S=UV^T三种参数化的动力学来揭示隐式偏置。

Result: 注意力参数化可充当架构隐式偏置。直接优化注意力矩阵S可能被困在无信息状态。绑定注意力诱导自动对称性破缺，可在Θ(d²log d)个样本下实现弱恢复。未绑定注意力则展现出快慢机制：预激活均值先在快时间尺度演化，重叠在慢时间尺度演化；当快动力学选择的状态打破初始对称性时，在Θ(d²log d)尺度上实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 不同注意力参数化方式具有本质不同的训练动力学和隐式偏置效应，这对理解注意力架构的学习效率和设计高效注意力机制具有指导意义。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [223] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"(equation recast)方法，将参数化算子学习重构为学习单一规范算子。参数引起的算子变化通过控制方程解析推导并吸收为有效源项，从而支持跨新参数域的零样本预测，并在多参数、非线性、奇异PDE设置中实现外推与多源异构数据整合，同时利用收敛失败作为内部预警信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化算子模型需要广泛覆盖输入函数和物理参数，导致数据需求量大；同时此类模型在训练分布之外可能静默失效（silently fail），缺乏可靠性与可推广性。

Method: 核心思想是将参数化算子学习"重铸"为单个规范算子（canonical operator）的学习。参数诱导的算子变化从控制方程中解析地导出，并吸收为有效源项（effective sources），从而将不同参数域的问题统一映射到规范域表示中。

Result: 在多参数、非线性及奇异PDE设定下，方程重铸支持跨参数外推，并能在共享规范表示中整合稀疏异构数据集。在核聚变高保真托卡马克仿真中，该框架通过规范域映射将四种设备几何结构下的电子温度数据统一到单个联合训练的算子中。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为实现可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条路径，兼具方程引导的迁移能力、数据效率和可监控的推理（利用收敛损失作为内部警告信号），提升了模型的可靠性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [224] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统调研了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习研究，从学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度梳理了基础原理，并对图数据场景提出了分布场景分类、统计异质性刻画、标准化问题表述和算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式机器学习面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制；现有协作学习（如联邦学习、去中心化学习）大多聚焦于欧几里得数据（图像、文本），难以捕捉实际应用中大量以图表示的关系模式。图学习依赖消息传递机制，与协作环境天然契合，但这一交叉领域的研究仍不充分，需要系统梳理。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：首先回顾协作学习在欧几里得数据上的基本原理，按学习有效性、效率、隐私保护三个维度进行组织；随后将讨论扩展到图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类学，刻画相关统计异质性，并建立标准化的问题表述和算法框架；最后系统识别开放性挑战和有前景的研究方向。

Result: 本文提供了一个从欧几里得到图结构数据的协作学习综合研究框架，包括三个核心维度的原理梳理、图分布场景分类法、统计异质性分析、标准化问题表述与算法框架，以及开放性挑战与研究方向的系统总结。

Conclusion: 协作学习在图结构数据上的研究处于早期阶段但潜力巨大，本文为该新兴领域提供了系统性的基础框架，明确了关键挑战和未来研究方向，有助于推动协作环境下图学习的发展。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [225] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于RoPE位置编码Transformer的头部功能组织，提出了两种干预指标（RFIS和RPD），揭示并验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学，其分界由训练长度的位置尺度决定（称为Global Positional Band）。据此提出两条原理（位置建模应仅局部进行、全局访问通过位置无关检索实现；两者应在头粒度按层分配），并实例化为Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH)，在低于1:3的FA/LA比例下取得优于Transformer、LA及层间混合基线的长上下文外推与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全注意力与线性注意力的混合架构分配方式仍依赖启发式规则，缺乏基于证据的分配依据。作者希望从RoPE Transformer中头部级别的功能组织中找到有实证支持的设计基础。

Method: 提出RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数，衡量各频率对注意力分布的影响）和RPD（RoPE位置依赖性，隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖）两种干预指标；在Qwen3系列与Llama3.1上揭示检索头与位置头的分类学；通过受控Transformer验证"Global Positional Band"边界随训练长度位置尺度变化；据此构建HwH（逐头混合架构）——NoPE FA承担全局检索，LA承担局部位置建模。

Result: RFIS与RPD在真实模型中验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学，发现存在显著的中低频带分界；该分界（GPBand）随训练长度位置尺度移动。HwH在FA:LA低于1:3的比例下，保持强语言建模与常识推理能力，同时改善了检索性能，并在零样本长上下文外推上显著强于Transformer、纯LA及层间混合基线。消融实验验证了两条原理及各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 混合注意力架构应遵循基于证据的头粒度设计原则而非启发式分配；位置建模限定局部、全局访问通过位置无关检索，并按头粒度分层分配，是面向未来基础模型的有前景路径。该分析还可能解释了零样本长度外推失败的根本原因。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [226] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上运行，预测每个网格节点的静电势及电子/空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量）。训练结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。该替代模型具备尺寸泛化能力（少鳍网格训练的模型可直接用于更大阵列），并通过深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习，在数秒内筛选海量候选设计并仅将最有信息量的样本送入完整仿真。在多重鳍三栅FinFET上对照Sentaurus Device基准，模型以亚伏每场RMSE复现三个漂移-扩散场，单设计吞吐量高出完整仿真数个数量级，且优势随器件尺寸增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散仿真虽仍是新兴FinFET器件设计的主力工具，但计算开销极大，尤其在3D结构中运行时随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习替代模型仅将固定设计参数映射到少量标量器件指标，丢弃了底层物理，且在不同器件几何与族类间缺乏迁移能力。

Method: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型：以四面体TCAD网格为图直接操作，在每一网格节点预测静电势与电子/空穴准费米能级。训练目标由数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差共同构成，将载流子输运物理嵌入损失函数；以图为载体继承尺寸泛化性；采用深度集成为每个节点提供不确定性估计，驱动主动学习循环，先快速筛选大候选池再仅仿真最有信息量的设计。

Result: 在多重鳍三栅FinFET上同Sentaurus Device对照，替代模型以亚伏每场RMSE准确复现静电势与电子/空穴准费米能级三个漂移-扩散场；单设计吞吐量比完整仿真高数个数量级。性能优势随器件尺寸增大而扩大：对于直接仿真过于缓慢的大型多鳍阵列，单器件推理仍可在1秒内完成，实现了直接TCAD扫描不可行的跨器件尺度帕累托前沿探索。

Conclusion: 该物理信息GAT替代模型通过直接在TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散场未知量并嵌入输运物理，兼具准确性、尺寸泛化性与高效推理能力，可与主动学习相结合，在数秒内完成大候选池筛选并仅对信息量最高的设计做完整仿真，从而实现了跨器件尺度上直接TCAD仿真无法企及的帕累托前沿设计空间探索。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [227] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出教师门控在线蒸馏（TGOPD），在提示层面验证教师可靠性后再决定是否采用密集监督，避免自信但错误的教师引发误导性更新；在4B和35B学生模型、数学/代码/指令遵循等多域任务中均优于Vanilla OPD，并大幅提升异步OPD中教师节点的GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPD对所有提示统一施加来自冻结教师的密集token级监督，未检查教师对每个提示是否可靠。由于反向KL具有模式寻求特性，自信但不正确的教师会诱导强烈却误导性的更新。已有的熵或师生似然一致性等分布性代理只能衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果的正确性。

Method: TGOPD遵循"教师可靠性应在提示层面、在密集监督注入之前被验证"的原则。先用少量经verifier评分的教师探针估计可靠性，再据此路由每个提示：可靠性检查通过时使用密集OPD监督，否则使用基于verifier的GRPO。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，覆盖数学、代码和指令遵循任务，TGOPD在所有六个单域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD，且在两种规模的多域训练下取得更高的七基准平均分。此外，TGOPD利用原本空闲的教师容量做可靠性估计，减少了异步OPD中教师侧计算浪费，使教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（实测4B单域运行）。

Conclusion: 在提示层面验证教师可靠性并将不可靠提示路由至verifier-grounded GRPO，是提升在线蒸馏效果与资源效率的有效策略，有望成为OPD范式下更稳健的通用改进方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [228] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench是一个基准框架，用于评估可解释性方法（内部估计器/观察者）是否足以指导下游干预、控制或安全任务。它分别报告估计准确性与行动引起的损失，并通过理论和实验证明两者需要分离评估。实验覆盖GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B等模型的电路干预和安全分诊任务，发现高准确率的观察者不一定产生低损失的行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性越来越多地被用来指导激活引导、电路移除和安全性监控等干预措施。然而，一个平均意义上准确的内部估计器仍可能选择糟糕的行动。现有评估往往只关注估计准确性，缺乏对下游行动效果的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出ObserverBench基准框架，为每项任务固定模型、信息边界、允许的行动、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数，分别报告估计准确性和基于所选行动导致的损失。在闭环控制中理论分析观察者错误在起点和干预可达方向上的影响；在电路干预任务中对比成对观察者与基于行动损失训练的观察者；在安全分诊中用AUROC与部署损失的对比评估监测器，并运行可复现基线和表格式提交。

Result: 在GPT-2-small和Qwen2.5-7B的电路干预任务中，成对观察者预测未见效应更准确但并非总能选择更好行动；基于行动损失训练的观察者能选择较低损失行动。在安全分诊中（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it、Qwen3.5-9B APPS），AUROC对监测器的排名可能与部署损失不同，最佳信息源因模型而异。稀疏SAE读数在Qwen面板上落后于对应的稠密控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench通过固定任务契约、可运行基线和表格式提交，为可解释性方法提供基于其实际启用的行动来评估的标准范式，推动可解释性方法从仅关注估计准确性转向关注下游决策效用。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [229] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出一种将预训练与迁移学习协同设计的方案：先在小子域上预训练神经算子，对于新的场景固定该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测，从而在多种域设置下无需大量高保真模拟即可实现低成本适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在面对与训练时不同的域几何、尺寸或工况时精度下降，监督式适应需要昂贵的高保真仿真数据，导致部署成本过高。现有方法未将预训练设计与迁移策略联合优化来降低目标域数据需求。

Method: LatentDDM先在小子域上预训练神经算子预测物理场。面对新的设置时，冻结该算子权重，仅训练一个轻量组合模块，将各子域的局部预测拼接组合成完整域的结果。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流中，与全域一次性处理的容量匹配模型相比，LatentDDM使用16个目标仿真数据适应更大的域后误差降低36-56%；在快速俯仰翼型的非定常不可压缩流动中，该方法改进了20步场滚动预测，无论是零样本还是小样本校准后均有效。

Conclusion: 局部预训练与组合级迁移的协同设计是物理基础模型的有前景的设计原则。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [230] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream提出了一种流式推测与精炼（speculate-and-refine）框架，用于设备端LLM推理，通过在GPU执行和存储I/O之间实现细粒度重叠，同时渐进式精炼计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，从而解决稀疏执行决策与高效I/O重叠之间的系统权衡。相比现有系统，LeanStream在保持最高吞吐的同时将内存占用降低4.8-7.5倍，并将token生成吞吐提升1.6-2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理虽具有隐私和响应性优势，但模型权重远超可用DRAM。现有系统虽利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载到SSD或闪存，却面临根本性系统权衡：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测，导致现有设计要么串行化执行，要么产生冗余权重获取、额外计算和大缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream采用流式推测-精炼框架，利用部分GPU结果渐进式地精炼计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，从而实现GPU执行与存储I/O之间的细粒度重叠。该系统在移动和嵌入式平台上均有实现。

Result: 相比先前的设备端LLM推理系统，LeanStream在达到先前工作最佳吞吐时将内存占用降低4.8倍至7.5倍，同时将token生成吞吐进一步提高1.6倍至2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过渐进式精炼策略成功破解了设备端LLM推理中稀疏决策准确性与计算-I/O重叠效率之间的矛盾，在降低内存占用的同时显著提升吞吐性能，为资源受限设备上的高效LLM推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [231] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究矩阵式连续思维链（matrix-CODI）模型中潜在矩阵Z的秩结构是否承载多条并行推理路径。通过rank-k投影消融实验、多种读出器（双线性、双线性+GELU、SVD增强、二次型）及线性探针等手段，发现所有rank-k曲线均平坦（精度差异≤0.6个百分点，Spearman p值均不显著），揭示该模型的推理在结构上对Z的秩不敏感；负对照实验表明rank-k消融本身混淆了"秩盲性"与"位置无关性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵值连续思维链模型中，每个潜在token经过d×d矩阵瓶颈编码。若矩阵潜在表示通过叠加承载并行推理路径，则秩应当反映这些路径的数量，且截断Z至低秩应损害需要多组件推理的任务精度。作者旨在验证这一假设以理解矩阵瓶颈的秩结构语义。

Method: 在四种训练配置（三种在ProsQA上，一种在低于学习阈值的GSM8K-Aug上）训练矩阵-CODI模型；执行rank-k投影消融（截断Z至低秩）；进行三种子重复实验；训练四种候选读出器（双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU非线性、SVD增强MLP、Z Z^T二次读出器）；使用线性探针比较目标预测性能；在无矩阵瓶颈的vanilla GPT-2 SFT上实施负对照实验，并引入random-h灵敏度下界。

Result: 所有训练配置下rank-k投影消融曲线平坦（波动≤0.6个百分点）；三种子重复精度81.0±2.0个百分点，而Z的有效秩跨{4,12,13}分布；四种非线性读出器下rank-k曲线均平坦（Spearman p值0.63、0.14、0.82、0.46）；线性探针低于原始预训练隐状态（AUC 0.673 vs 0.846）；GPT-2负对照复现平坦曲线（合并均值范围0.20pp），random-h灵敏度下界达到相同精度水平。

Conclusion: 矩阵-CODI模型的推理在结构上对潜在矩阵Z的秩不敏感，秩盲性不源于flatten-then-project读出器这一特定架构选择。然而，负对照实验表明rank-k消融作为研究手段不足以区分秩盲性与位置无关性，因为该干预在无矩阵瓶颈的普通模型上同样产生平坦曲线。结论需要更精细的干预方法以分离这两个混淆因素。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [232] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用深度光流替代传统窗口相关跟踪方法，用于对地静止卫星（GEO）大气运动矢量（AMV）的立体匹配检索，并通过知识蒸馏将多卫星立体教师模型压缩为单卫星学生模型，实现全球全圆盘成像的高效风场反演。验证表明立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9、7.3 μm）优于业务AMV，但在长波红外（11.2 μm）波段存在性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV反演依赖窗口交叉相关进行特征跟踪，并通过红外亮温结合数值天气预报背景场估计高度，造成循环依赖、高度不准确、计算成本高和检索稀疏等问题。虽然GEO-GEO和GEO-LEO立体风技术可通过视差位移几何解析高度，消除对NWP的依赖并提高精度，但计算开销大且覆盖范围有限。

Method: 在立体匹配中用深度光流替代窗口相关跟踪以实现高效改进的反演；微调过程平衡自监督几何残差损失与有监督探空仪重建损失；通过知识蒸馏将立体教师模型压缩为单卫星学生模型；学生模型模拟教师的卡方和高度不确定性进行质量控制；学生模型在GEO全圆盘图像上生成全球风场。

Result: 通过三重搭配分析验证，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9、7.3 μm）上的性能优于业务AMV，但长波红外（11.2 μm）波段的表现有退化。验证对比了探空仪、业务AMV、ERA5再分析和EarthCARE云廓线。

Conclusion: 深度光流与知识蒸馏相结合的方案能够在不依赖NWP背景场的情况下实现高效、高覆盖的风场反演，且在水汽波段的精度超过业务运行系统，为下一代卫星风场反演提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [233] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了现代深度学习中的规模定律（scaling laws）是否同样适用于精算费率厘定场景。在真实机动车保险组合数据上，作者比较了多种模型族在不同训练数据比例下的泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）表现，发现所有模型族均随数据量增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM模型展现出远强于Transformer和MLP的数据缩放能力，而Transformer若不引入额外归纳偏置（如TabM式适应或自监督），其参数缩放表现较弱。结果表明，精算表格任务的缩放效能取决于架构设计与损失函数设计，单纯增大Transformer规模收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的规模定律已被广泛验证于图像、语言等领域，但精算费率厘定等表格数据任务是否呈现类似的规律尚不清楚。精算数据具有表格化、异质性强、噪声大的特点，且GLM等经典模型仍是强基线。理解不同模型族在精算任务上的数据与参数缩放行为，可为按数据规模选择模型提供定量指导。

Method: 使用真实机动车保险组合数据，训练多种模型族（TabM、纯监督表格Transformer、标准MLP基线及其增强变体），在不同比例的增量训练数据及多个随机种子下重复实验，以样本外泊松偏差（越低越好）作为评估指标，分析各模型族的缩放指数与数据/参数规模之间的关系。

Result: 所有模型族的样本外表现均随数据量增加而提升，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM的数据缩放能力明显优于纯监督表格Transformer和标准MLP基线。Transformer变体在参数缩放方面表现弱，除非辅以额外归纳偏置（TabM式适应或自监督预训练）。简单的Transformer规模扩大仅带来有限的性能增益。

Conclusion: 精算表格任务上确实存在缩放宽泛规律，但缩放效率高度依赖于架构选择与损失函数/目标设计。模型应依据可用数据规模进行选择，单纯增加Transformer参数量并不是提升精算预测性能的有效策略，未来工作应关注归纳偏置和训练目标的设计以增强表格任务上的缩放能力。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [234] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经场（NF）从稀疏观测中快速高质量重建的难题，提出了三种算法：NTK-KIP通过蒸馏支持集实现有限NTK对大缺失区域的修复；MetaQuill通过元学习共享初始化，仅更新少量任务特定权重偏移即可适应新场景；融合两者的MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下实现高PSNR重建与语义合理修复，且只需轻量级逐实例适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场将连续坐标映射为色彩或密度等信号，但从稀疏观测中进行快速高质量重建仍然困难。经典NTK回归虽能闭式拟合，但本质上是线性的，无法积累可复用的任务先验，这一局限阻碍了神经场在线性核方法与实用少样本重建之间架起桥梁。

Method: 提出三种算法：1) NTK-KIP学习一个蒸馏后的支持集（坐标及可选标签），使有限NTK能从少量观测数据修复大面积缺失区域，从而形成紧凑的非线性表示而非原始核求解；2) MetaQuill对隐式神经表示（INR）元学习一个共享初始化，使新场景仅通过更新一个小的任务特定权重偏移即可适配，实现真正的特征学习与可复用先验；3) MetaQuill-KIP融合两者，先以KIP风格的非线性热启动种子初始化任务，再围绕元学习初始化仅精修这一小偏移量。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下达到了高PSNR重建性能并实现了语义合理的修复结果，同时仅需轻量级逐实例适配，相比依赖大型预训练生成先验且需要昂贵逐图调优的扩散式基线更具效率优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明NTK驱动的神经场可以被同时赋予非线性和元学习能力，缩小了解析核方法与实用少样本重建之间的差距，为神经场的少样本重建开辟了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [235] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"MoE路由的token级分离即可防止域间负迁移"这一常见假设，指出即使专家路由近不相交，多域LoRA微调仍会出现性能干扰。作者通过诊断指标定位干扰源于同一低秩适配器子空间内近似正交的域梯度竞争，并提出SpawnLoRA（在MoE专家内部动态添加带门控的子适配器），在多个模型与混合设置上有效减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域微调常将MoE路由与LoRA结合，默认假设token级路由可将域特定更新分开而不互相干扰。作者通过实验检测并挑战该假设，发现路由分离本身并不能防止负迁移，因此需要定位失败原因并提出解决方案。

Method: （1）以Python代码、生物医学文本和数学推理为测试域，结合路由重叠与损失分析检验MoE+LoRA假设；（2）提出Jaccard路由重叠（衡量专家共享程度）与适配器梯度余弦相似度（衡量更新兼容性）两种诊断指标；（3）提出SpawnLoRA，在检测到适配器级冲突时于MoE专家内部动态添加门控子适配器，保持路由器固定。

Result: 实验表明即使各域专家路由近不相交，加入生物医学数据仍显著提高代码困惑度，证实负迁移存在；诊断显示干扰主要源于近似正交的域梯度在同一低秩适配器子空间内竞争。SpawnLoRA在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B上多组混合设置下有效降低负迁移，优于标准LoRA和rank-adaptive LoRA。

Conclusion: 专家内部的结构性分离相比单纯的路由分离或秩扩展能带来额外收益，说明缓解跨域干扰需要在适配器子空间层面提供隔离机制，而不只是依赖路由或增大秩。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [236] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型（LLM）作为批量优化器在连续和离散设置中的表现，发现LLM在数值测试函数上是具有竞争力的零样本批量优化器，但性能较经典非LLM方法更为脆弱；然而在语义丰富的离散空间中，LLM展现出显著优势，表明其批量优化行为在与其预训练数据结构相似的场景中尤为高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM凭借大规模预训练已成为有吸引力的优化先验，但其作为现代推理型LLM在批量优化场景中的有效性尚未得到充分探索，因此需要系统评估其在连续和离散两种设定下的表现。

Method: 将当前一代前沿LLM作为零样本批量优化器，分别在连续（数值测试函数）和离散（语义丰富场景）两种优化设定下进行系统性评估，并与经典非LLM优化方法进行对比。

Result: LLM在数值测试函数上是具有竞争力的零样本批量优化器，但其性能稳定性不如经典非LLM方法；而在语义丰富的设定中，LLM先验显著优于其他方法。

Conclusion: LLM的批量优化能力高度依赖于优化空间与其预训练数据的结构相似性——在与预训练数据语义相似的离散空间中表现优异，但在纯数值空间中稳健性不足。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [237] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地验证了DECAF框架（通过因果图边切割实现公平性）不限于其最初的非私有GAN机制，而是可迁移至九种来自三个不同家族（基于边际分布、GAN和扩散模型）的生成器（各含差分隐私变体），并在2520组匹配运行次中验证于Adult和COMPAS数据集上。实验证明该机制普遍有效，且新提出的因果扩散主干在所有家族中取得了最公平的发布，保真度接近边际分布层；施加切割对保真度影响极小，仅使下游分类器AUC平均下降0.07至0.15，且隐私保证不会降低数据公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DECAF将三种公平性定义转化为生成器因果图上的三组边切割，但其仅在一个非私有GAN上进行了验证，尚不清楚这种机制是DECAF特有还是因果分解本身的通用性质。本文旨在检验该公平性机制在不同生成器家族与隐私条件下的普适性。

Method: 将DECAF的三种公平性定义应用到九种来自三个不相关家族的生成器上：基于边际分布的生成器、GAN和扩散模型，每种均配置差分隐私变体，横跨三个形式化隐私保证级别。共执行2520组匹配对运行，使用Adult和COMPAS数据集进行系统比较，并提出一种新的因果扩散生成主干。

Result: 公平性边切割机制在全部测试的生成器家族中均有效迁移；新提出的因果扩散主干在所有测试家族中产生了最公平的发布，其保真度接近边际分布层水平。施加边切割对保真度几乎没有影响，仅使下游分类器的AUC平均下降0.07至0.15，而添加隐私保证并不会使数据变得更不公平。

Conclusion: 公平性-因果分解机制具有跨家族普适性，属于因果分解本身而非DECAF特有方法。因果扩散生成器在公平与保真之间取得了最优权衡，且差分隐私与公平性可以同时实现而不相互损害。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [238] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioGlyph，一种将网络拓扑编译为可解释且可迁移的结构角色语言的表示方法，能显著提升开源大语言模型回答结构推理问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言任务中表现出色，但当网络以边列表、句子或测量表形式呈现时，难以理解其结构含义（如节点中心性、社区桥接等），需要一种介于网络拓扑与LLM之间的可解释、可迁移的结构化表示。

Method: BioGlyph结合图划分(structural partitioning)与结构测量来识别网络中各元素的结构角色（如枢纽hub、社区核心、跨社区连接器），并通过固定规则将这些角色翻译成通用词汇表。每种元素用其结构角色、支持证据和语义后果来描述，网络与LLM本身均无需改动。

Result: 在横跨五个领域的二十个网络上，BioGlyph显著提升开源LLM回答结构推理问题的能力，相比基于边、数值和学习的表示，系统准确率最多提升26个百分点。消融实验表明增益来自显式编码语义可解释的结构角色；增益在密集、社区结构明显的网络中更为突出，在稀疏网络中增益减弱。在芽殖酵母蛋白相互作用网络中，BioGlyph揭示了生物学组织性：跨社区连接器富集于必需基因，而外围蛋白则表现为缺失。

Conclusion: BioGlyph为语言模型和科学家提供了一种可解释且语义化地推理网络结构的表示方法，既提高了机器推理能力又保留了人类可理解性。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles.
